2026. 10.27
先週（10/19～10/26）発表の主な経済指標
米生産活動は堅調。AI関連投資を背景にコンピューター・電子
製品や機械などがけん引。一方、データセンター建設に伴う需要
増加が、他産業の生産や建設活動を圧迫する兆候も
コア消費者物価は再び2％超え。特殊要因を除くサービス価格
も加速し、人件費転嫁の兆し。連合は2027年春闘で6％中心
を要求。中小企業の余力に課題も、物価上振れに注意
注目点② サービス物価の加速で重み増す2027年春闘戦線注目点①AI投資の恩恵拡大、副作用もじわり顕在化
総合PMIは57.6と高水準を維持。新規失業保険申
請件数も19.4万件と低水準で解雇の動きは引き続
き限定的。一方で、住宅着工件数や住宅建設業
者の景況感は悪化、高金利の影響が継続

耐久財製造業は、軍需やデータセンター（DC）増
設の旺盛な需要が、他の製品の生産を圧迫して
いると報告ベージュブック
（2026/10） テクノロジー企業がDC向け電気設備の買い占め
を試みたが、供給業者は他案件への供給を優先
し、これを拒否
機械製造業は、DC向けに鋼材在庫が大量に消

の建設案件の遅延を招いていると指摘
テキサス州
見通し調査
（2026/9）
製造業では原材料コストが上昇。特に鋼材価格
が急上昇しており、その一因はDC需要とされるベージュブック
（2026/9） 一部の製造業がアルミニウム・銅・鋼材の供給不
足や調達期間の長期化を報告
一部の自動車メーカーはアルミニウムや一部部品
の供給不足で生産が幾分抑制されたと報告

ベージュブック
（2026/7）
米国：AI関連を中心に製造業生産は堅調も、資材・設備の需給ひっ迫が他産業に波及
 米国の製造業生産は、AI関連投資を背景にコンピューター・電子製品、一次金属、一般機械などが堅調
― 一方、非耐久財や家具など消費関連の生産は軟調に推移し、業種間のばらつきが拡大
 FRBは、データセンター建設に伴う鋼材・電気設備などの需要増加が、他の建設案件や生産活動に影響していると指摘
―AI投資の拡大が関連産業の生産を押し上げる一方、供給制約を通じて他産業の活動を圧迫する兆候も
― 当面はAI投資による成長押し上げ効果が期待される一方、供給制約や価格上昇を通じた負の影響にも注意が必要
4

日本：全国コアCPIは再び2％超え。基調的なサービス価格も伸び高まる
5
 9月の全国コアCPIは前年比＋2.1％（前月：同＋1.7％）と加速。2025年12月以来の2％超に
― 原油高・円安等のコスト上昇もあり、家事用消耗品や教養娯楽用品等を中心に伸び率が拡大
― 一般サービス価格も伸び加速。シルバーウィークの並びによる宿泊料や外食費の上昇などが主因
 特殊要因を除いた一般サービス価格もじわり上昇
― 変動の大きい宿泊費や私立高校無償化による押し下げを除いた基調的な一般サービス価格に上昇圧力。人件費増の
価格転嫁が進んでいる可能性を示唆

全国コアCPI CPI一般サービス価格の推移

### Page 7

日本：連合は2027年春闘で賃上げ要求を強化。高水準の賃上げ持続と物価への波及が焦点
6
 連合は、2027年春闘賃上げ要求の目安を「6％中心、5％以上」と発表
―5％以上の賃上げを最低目標として明確化。実質賃金1％上昇の定着をめざす方針
― 中小企業は大企業との格差是正に向け「6％以上」が目標。過去4年のインフレで実質賃金が低下した分の回復も要求
― また、新たに、2030年に2026年の賃金水準を15％程度上回る中期目標を設定。持続的な賃金上昇をめざす
 賃上げ余力は企業規模・業種で濃淡。中小企業等で高い「賃上げ5％の壁」
― なお、6％に近い賃上げが実現する場合、サービス価格への波及が強まり、基調的物価上昇率が2％を上回る可能性
連合・春闘基本構想の新旧比較 春闘賃上げ率

### Page 8

欧州：2027年度予算審議が本格化。生活苦によるデモが拡大する中、財政赤字は高水準
7
 2027年度予算審議が本格化。財政赤字は総じて高止まり
― 一定の防衛費・エネルギー関連支出を柔軟化する特例措置の適用後も、複数の国でEU財政基準で定める赤字幅を超
過。スペインは予算案を巡って議会解散。フランス・ベルギーも10/15時点でEUに予算案を未提出
 生活改善を求める抗議活動が欧州各国で発生。支出圧力の高まりは財政再建の障壁に
― フランスの学生によるデモを発端に、生活費の高騰や公共サービスの劣化に対する抗議活動は欧州各国に拡大。一部
は暴動に発展し、警察官と衝突する事態に
9～10月に発生した主な抗議活動主要国の財政収支実績と見通し
（出所） 各種報道より、みずほ総合研究所調査部作成

欧州：金融市場は政治・財政を巡る混乱を嫌気。不透明感は2027年入り後も継続する見込み
8
 金融市場は政治・財政を巡る混乱を警戒。主要国の選挙を控える中、不透明感は2027年まで継続へ
― 抗議活動が広がるイタリア、フランス、ベルギーの一般政府債務残高（GDP比）は、EU加盟国の中でも上位（それぞれ2
位、3位、4位）。金利上昇の背景は政治・財政への懸念
― 影響は為替市場にも波及。9月中旬以降、ユーロはドルに対して弱含み
―2027年にかけてフランス大統領選やスペイン・イタリアの総選挙など、欧州主要国で重要な国政選挙が相次ぐ。極右・ポ

Cathay’s View市場策略思維
市場焦點
1. 市場焦點:
• 中東戰火升溫, WTI高位震盪: 伊朗加大船隻攻擊, 胡塞攻擊沙國, WTI一度站上百元大關。傳聞俄烏露
出談判曙光, 油價自高點回落, 但運費高漲, 研判油價仍將高位整理。
• 聯準會10月可望維持利率不變, 美債利率保持高位: 中東局勢升溫與AI融資疑慮升高, 資金流入美債避
險, 加上官員釋出觀望訊號, 美債殖利率自高點回落, 但通膨壓力仍在, 使美債利率高檔震盪。
• AI需求依舊強勁, 但企業獲利動能高峰已過: 超微電腦、SpaceX及Meta均釋出AI需求強勁的訊號, 緩解
AI泡沫疑慮, 不過, 考量獲利增速高峰已過, 可分散佈局, 降低市場波動壓力。
2. 後續看點:
• 3大CSP財報(10/27-10/28)、FOMC利率會議(10/29)、ECB政策會議(10/29)、台灣GDP(10/30)
策略思維
1. 股市−多頭不變, 但高利率增添評價波動: 殖利率高位, 評價面持續承壓, 使股價落後基本面表現。不過, 企業

營收獲利未來成長空間仍大, 股市仍有高點可期, 惟獲利成長高峰已過, 盡量分散各產業。
2. 債市−長端不確定性仍高, 宜以中短天期或公司債為佳: 通膨高於預期, 加上政府債務壓力, 長率仍有上行壓
力, 建議控制債券部位存續期間, 選擇短天期或浮動債較佳。
3. 匯市−美元走揚與股市震盪, 引發亞幣走弱: 美元雙率走高提高外資賣超亞股, 亞幣偏弱看待。

主要中東油國產油持續回升 航運運費居高不下
原油｜主要中東油國產油雖持續回升, 但航運運費居高不下、墊高原油成本, 讓油價難明顯回落
• 中東主要產油國的原油生產逐步修復 , 包括沙烏地阿拉伯 、阿聯等國生產持續回升 。這意味著全球原油供給端的
緊縮壓力已較上個季度改善。從供需平衡的角度來看, 產油回升理應為油價帶來下行空間。
• 然而, 運輸環節卻抵消上述供給改善的影響。中東航道的安全風險持續存在,部分油輪則改道好望角, 種種因素均推
升運費水準。高昂的航運成本成為原油到岸價格的重要墊高因素, 使得即使產地價格出現鬆動, 最終到達消費地的
成本仍居高不下。這種「產地供給改善、運抵成本居高」落差, 讓市場難以單純透過產量回升來消化價格壓力。
• 供給回升與運費高漲、地緣風險難解的交互作用下, 原油價格較難明顯回落。油價可能延續高位震盪, 並對全球通
膨預期與央行政策形成牽制。

資料來源: Bloomberg, 截至2026/10/20。 資料來源: Bloomberg, 截至2026/10/20。

中東戰爭以來, 中國原油進口下降 今年以來, 中國油品與塑料出口價格走升
原油｜中國原油進口下降, 雖有助舒緩原油供需緊俏, 但禁止油品出口, 卻讓需求國油品價格難降
• 中國作為全球最大原油進口國 , 中東戰爭以來進口量有所回落 (因先前低油價時期購入大量被制裁原油 , 積累高達
12~14億桶庫存, 因此戰後選擇不當高價油的接受者), 為原油供需緊張局面注入一絲緩解空間, 並在一定程度上抑
制原油現貨價格的上行動能。從宏觀供需平衡來看, 這本應為原油市場帶來溫和的修正空間。
• 然而, 中國10月實施油品出口限制, 在下游成品環節形成不同影響。受到戰爭影響, 中東部分煉油、裂解產能受到
影響, 加上美國產輕甜油但多數煉油與裂解產能適合以重油提煉, 使得對煉油價差、油品等報價難降。雪上加霜的

是, 中國10月初宣布, 因應中東局勢與國內庫存偏低, 暫停成品油外銷, 使整體成品油市場更形緊俏。
• 整體來說,「原油轉鬆、成品仍緊」的結構性背離持續, 對消費者能源價格的僵固性將更為明顯, 也對仰賴進口成品
油的經濟體影響更為艱難。
資料來源: Bloomberg, 截至2026/10/20。 資料來源: Bloomberg, 截至2026/10/20。
萬噸 Index (2020/1 =100) Index (2020/1 =100)

短線升息時點遞延, 後續緊縮方向未改 預估10月維持不變, 今年底、明年Q1各升息一碼
資料來源: Bloomberg, 國泰世華銀行整理, 截至2026/10/16。 資料來源: Bloomberg預估, 截至2026/10/20。
• 10月FOMC料按兵不動, 未來將進一步緊縮: 9月升息一碼後, 近期Fed官員普遍認為無須急於再次升息, Fed將持續
觀察貨幣政策對物價控制、金融狀況的影響; 惟後續升息路徑尚未結束。
• 能源衝擊外溢, 物價壓力支撐後續升息路徑: 美國9月CPI月增0.6%、PPI月增1.3%, 能源供應短缺的衝擊逐步擴散。
褐皮書亦顯示, 柴油、燃料成本正沿著供應鏈傳導, 使原物料、關稅、保險成本持續推升企業價格壓力。
• 短線升息預期降溫, 反映時點遞延: 預料12月升息一碼, 明年Q1亦保留再升一碼空間; 而明年3月將受高基期影響,
有助通膨數據(年增)呈現降溫。
FOMC前瞻｜預期10月Fed按兵不動, 年底升息可能性仍高

官員 評論
Kevin Warsh
(主席, 10/16)
不預判10、12月路徑, 透過任務小組以研議政策框架 。
重申物價穩定仍為核心, 同時關注AI投資需求帶來的
通膨、金融穩定風險。

通膨仍高於目標, 9月升息後無需急於行動, 可先觀察

殖利率創近年新高後, 長債買盤回溫、曲線趨平 財政赤字惡化、公債供給壓力延續
資料來源: Bloomberg, 截至2026/10/20。 資料來源: Bloomberg, 截至2026/10/14。
• 10月升息預期降溫, 前期債市賣壓暫獲舒緩: 先前較激進的升息路徑逐漸修正, 以及資金對美債的避險偏好回升, 帶
動殖利率自高點回落。另方面, 隨殖利率升至相對高檔, 亦增添債券配置吸引力: 10/9拍賣10年期美國公債需求強
勁, 投標倍數2.77倍創近十年來高點, 並且直接投標人、間接投標人合計獲配比例97.4%創史上新高。
• 縱使近期買盤回流, 但整體「利率高原」格局未改: 1)中東衝突與原油供給風險, 使通膨及Fed升息路徑仍具不確定
性; 2)美國財政赤字、公債供給壓力延續, 加上AI投資推升企業發債需求, 結構性因素仍限制長端利率下行空間。

市場評估AI融資風險, 近期信用利差再度走擴 槓桿率較高AI業者利率走升
註: 圖表以2026/1=100標準化, 目前利差IG=86bps、HY=332bps、CCC=1085bps。
資料來源: Bloomberg, 截至2026/10/20。
註: 槓桿率採Net Debt/EBITDA, CoreWeave約為9.5x, Oracle為3.8x, Hyperscalers僅介於
-0.7x~0.7x。
資料來源: Bloomberg, 截至2026/10/20。
• 近期公債利率快速下滑, 信用債利率同步回落, 惟降幅相對有限導致信用利差走擴。另方面, 在融資成本升高下, 市
場持續檢視AI業者的ROI, 以及營收、現金流能否覆蓋龐大Capex及利息支出, 成為信用風險的定價關鍵。
• 壓力集中於高槓桿業者: 大型Hyperscalers具較強內部現金流, 近期壓力集中在高度依賴外部融資、槓桿率更高的
業者; CoreWeave信用疑慮升高(債務規模龐大、Q2淨虧損), Oracle FCF承壓、市場亦擔憂降評風險。

• AI投資的雙向信用風險: AI Capex維持高檔, 持續推升企業發債與融資需求; 但更大尾端風險在於, 若Hyperscalers
下修資本支出並減少外部算力需求, 雖有助改善自身FCF, 但將削弱Neocloud、資料中心等租賃算力業者的訂單。
• 債市布局: 高利率提升債券配置吸引力, 惟發行人分化加劇, 體質仍以IG為主、報酬著眼於穩定息收。

主要企業財報狀況
註: 超微電腦目前只公布財報初稿, 完成財報將於11/3公布。
資料來源: Bloomberg, , 國泰世華銀行整理, 截至2026Q3財報。
• 受到油價走揚、CoreWeave融資利率飆升等因素影響, 市場對AI泡沫的疑慮升溫, 科技股明顯回檔。不過, 隨著主
要科技企業陸續公布財報, 包括超微電腦、SpaceX 及 Meta 均釋出 AI 需求強勁的訊號, 帶動科技股逐步回升。
• 其中, SpaceX 本季營收達161億美元, 年增161%, 主要成長動能來自AI業務的爆發式成長, 營收從去年同期的8.1
億美元大幅攀升至75.6億美元, 甚至超越Starlink的連網業務。此外, 美國AI伺服器大廠超微電腦也受惠於強勁的
AI需求, 最新季度營收初步預估中位數達190億美元, 顯示 AI基礎建設需求仍具強勁動能。

獲利動能變動下的標普500表現 獲利動能變化下各類股表現
註: 標普500企業當季獲利年增率若高於上季, 表示加速, 反之為減速。
資料來源: Bloomberg, 從2000Q1-2026Q2。
註: 標普500企業當季獲利年增率若高於上季, 表示加速, 反之為減速。
資料來源: Bloomberg, 從2000Q1-2026Q2。
• 標普500企業財報陸續開跑, 銀行股已率先公布財報, 整體表現大致符合市場預期, 接下來市場焦點將轉向10月底
陸續公布財報的大型雲端服務供應商(CSP)。受惠於AI熱潮延續, 市場預期標普500企業Q3獲利仍將維持亮眼表現,
年增率可望達到30%。
• 值得留意的是, 儘管AI熱潮持續, 但隨著獲利基期墊高, 企業獲利成長動能的高峰可能已過, 後續增速恐難超越Q2
的50%。從過去經驗來看, 當企業獲利成長逐步放緩, 股市未來報酬率也可能隨之下降。此外, 在高利率環境下, 股
市波動風險仍不容忽視, 投資配置不妨適度提高防禦型類股比重, 包括核心消費與公用事業, 以提升投資組合的防

禦能力。

記憶體｜回顧過去30年十次記憶體循環, 股價約領先獲利峰值一季見頂
資料來源: Bloomberg, 國泰世華銀行整理, 以上數據均為美光財報與股價表現, 截至2026/10/19。
• 根據歷史規律, 即便獲利成長率已見頂, 若獲利絕對金額仍能持續成長, 記憶體股價仍能夠持續創高。然而, 一旦獲

利金額見頂, 該波週期股價漲勢多半提前結束。過去週期股價平均約領先獲利3個月見頂, 換言之, 市場目前的定價
隱含記憶體廠的獲利將於2026Q3至Q4後開始季減。
成長率見頂, 漲勢仍有可能延續很久 獲利一旦見頂, 漲勢提前結束

DRAM廠商2027年產能擴增幅度有限 DRAM報價仍持續上漲
記憶體｜2027供給新增有限, 若AI需求並未放緩, 供不應求格局暫難扭轉
• 然而, 美光先前給出的樂觀展望與市場的預期出現明顯落差, 公司稱2027與2028的供需情形將較2026年更加嚴峻,
且FY27第一季度(2026/11)的毛利率將是FY27全年毛利率的地板 。另一方面, 目前記憶體合約價與現貨價仍持續
上漲, 需求並未出現明顯轉折。
• 即便2028年以後的AI需求是否放緩, 記憶體供不應求情形是否改變的確仍具備不確定性 , 但2027年各廠商新增產
能有限, 同時算力需求仍然強勁, 明年記憶體廠商獲利出現衰退的機率較低, 近期的劇烈回檔更偏向情緒驅動而非
基本面改變。
資料來源: J.P. Morgan預估, 截至2026/8/9。 資料來源: Bloomberg, 截至2026/10/1。

日本出口維持雙位數, 惟經濟表現略有放緩 BOJ料維持緩步升息
資料來源: Bloomberg, 截至2026/9。 資料來源: Bloomberg預估, 截至2026/10/21。
• 日本9月出口年增16.7%, 連13個月維持正成長, 不過9月出口增速連兩月放緩, 也低於市場預期的20.1%, 搭配花旗
日本經濟驚奇指數自高檔回落, 顯示經濟成長動能略有放緩, 但仍呈現穩健成長。
• 近期高市首相在國會接受質詢時否認政策導致日圓疲弱和推高日本利率, 表示會適當地控制政府發債規模, 日本10
年債殖利率自3.1%高點回落至2.9%下方, 但仍是近30年來最高水準, 且央行總裁植田和男維持穩健升息論調, 預料
將於12月再度升息, 使日債殖利率維持相對高檔。

法國民眾關注焦點 法國信用持續惡化
資料來源: Bloomberg, IPSOS, 截至2026/9。 資料來源: Bloomberg, 截至2026/10/21。
歐洲｜民怨烽火且預算卡關, 法債難平
• 法國政府試圖推動溫和減赤 , 卻在極左與極右陣營反對下面臨極高的倒閣危機 。信評機構穆迪於10月23日公布的
評級審查, 預料法國原有的「Aa3」評等面臨被直接調降至 「A1」的風險, 恐推升融資成本。此外10/27預算收入
案若再拖延, 法國10年期公債殖利率與法德利差恐持續惡化。
• 與此同時, 社會民怨也已達到臨界點, 今年酷暑熱浪重創核電供給, 導致民生管制電價在8月被迫調升2.5%, 進一步
剝奪了實質購買力 。民眾普遍認為惡化的公共服務與斷裂的福利合約 , 導致「深知債務迫切、卻拒絕承擔緊縮 」
的認知撕裂, 催生了10月中旬高達數十萬人的全國性暴力街頭抗爭。高昂的電費與生活成本積怨, 正與政治財政危

機相互綁定, 使法國陷入難以解套的惡性循環, 短期法債風險難以消除。

歐元區交易區間下移 歐美12月會議利率變動機率
資料來源: Bloomberg, 截至2026/10/21。 資料來源: Bloomberg, 截至2026/10/21。
匯市｜歐元受事件衝擊後回神, 年底利率變動預期仍是關鍵
• 歐元兌美元跌破1.14並下移至新的交易區間後 , 目前呈現低檔震盪築底的態勢 。儘管法國國會僵局與穆迪即將到
來的降評風險等結構性問題依舊, 但市場在經歷先前的恐慌拋售後, 已對此利空進行了定價。畢竟歐元是集合體且
有德國托底, 隨著政治風暴對匯市的邊際影響逐漸減弱, 歐美兩大央行12月會議的「利差變動預期」將接棒成為驅
動歐元後續走勢的核心力道。
• 從近期市場預期來看 , 歐洲央行與聯準會雙方對貨幣政策路徑的角力正進入白熱化 。以目前通膨數據以及雙方官
員的表態, 12月利差難以擴大, 歐元可能繼續受制政治雜音於低位震盪, 但無序下跌的機率有限, 短期先看重回區間
上緣走勢。

外資創紀錄賣超台股, 為本波新台幣大貶主因 股市何時回穩, 為新台幣後續觀察指標
資料來源: Bloomberg, 截至2026/10/21。 資料來源: Bloomberg, 截至2026/10/21。
• 科技股拋售潮持續, 10月外資猛烈倒貨台股, 單月已賣超7416億元, 創下歷史新高。資金回撤持續使新台幣承壓 ,
為本波匯率重貶主因。

影響提高。若台股止穩反彈、美債殖利率回落、AI科技股拋售行情告終, 均有助亞幣止貶。

市場焦點
美國
• 10月S&P Global綜合PMI初值由58.4降至57.6，雖較前月回落，但仍處高檔區間；服務業動
能略為放緩，製造業PMI則升至56.0，創近4年半新高。新訂單增速加快 ，顯示企業需求、投
資及庫存回補動能仍具韌性 ，然而企業售價與投入成本同步上升 ，反映需求改善 、供應鏈延
遲及成本轉嫁效應持續存在 ，通膨降溫進程仍具不確定性 。整體而言 ，市場關注焦點轉向通
膨與利率走勢，高利率環境恐維持較長時間，進而推高股債資產評價波動。
中國
• 中國國家統計局公布第三季 GDP年增率4.5%，優於市場預期 ，在出口與科技製造業帶動下 ，
短期成長可望維持穩定 ，但若內需未能有效改善 ，經濟成長動能恐難全面擴散 ，建議聚焦在
半導體、AI硬體、伺服器供應鏈 ，及高端製造相關產業 ，至於消費 、房地產及傳統內需類股 ，

仍需觀察後續刺激政策與消費信心回升情況。
法國
政局動盪
• 法國金融市場因各地抗議活動持續擴大 ，面臨股債雙跌的局面。市場憂心法國政府可能擴大支
出以平息抗議，加劇財政可持續性疑慮 。法國10年期公債殖利率一度突破 5%，法德利差亦擴
大至歐債危機以來高點。不過，歐洲央行系統性壓力綜合指標仍處於歷史相對低檔 ，遠低於歐
元區主權債務危機期間，短期內演變為新一輪主權債務危機的風險仍相對有限。

美國PMI維持強勁擴張，經濟成長續航但通膨壓力再升溫
• 企業需求強勁支撐美國景氣維持高檔擴張 ：美國10月綜合PMI初值由58.4降至57.6，雖略低於前月，但仍處於近五年來
的高位區間，顯示企業活動持續高速擴張；其中服務業 PMI為57.9、製造業PMI升至56.0並創53個月新高，新訂單增速
更創逾4年半最佳表現，反映內需穩健、出口需求改善與企業補庫存效應同步發酵，美國第四季經濟動能仍具韌性。
• 成本上升與供應瓶頸推升通膨風險： 企業售價漲幅創2022年8月以來最高，能源、運輸、金屬原料及薪資成本同步上揚 ，
加上供應鏈交期延長與訂單積壓擴大，使企業得以持續轉嫁成本，暗示未來數月消費者通膨可能再度偏離聯準會目標區
間，市場對利率維持較高水準的預期恐因此升溫。
• 後市觀察：景氣韌性有利獲利成長，但通膨可能推升殖利率、壓抑高評價資產。投資配置建議優先布局美國大型科技股 、

美國金融股及工業資本支出受惠族群，可透過NASDAQ 100 ETF、S&P 500 ETF及美國銀行股ETF參與企業獲利成長；
固定收益則以短天期投資級債為主，以降低利率波動風險。
資料來源：Bloomberg， 2026.10.24

海外資金流入美國市場，支撐美國資產吸引力
• 海外資金持續流入美國：美國財政部8月TIC資料顯示，
整體資金淨流入 1,998億美元，其中民間部門淨流入
1,785億美元，為主要資金來源；外資淨買進美國長
期證券3,007億美元，遠高於美國投資人加碼海外資
產216億美元，反映全球資金在經濟成長與流動性考
量下，對美國金融市場仍維持高度的配置需求。
• 全球資金偏好美國資產： 外資 8月淨買入美國股票
1,319億美元，高於美國公債與公司債 ，顯示海外投
資人仍看好美國企業獲利與經濟基本面 ，雖然短期國
庫券持有量下降，部分跨境美元資金亦出現波動，但
以10月近況來看長天期公債標售仍吸引境外央行 、
保險資金及大型資產管理機構參與，反映高殖利率水
準對長期資金仍具吸引力。在經濟成長維持韌性與企

業獲利支撐下，美國資產仍為全球資金配置的重要核
心市場。
• 市場看法：海外資金流入有助反映美國資產吸引力，
惟後續仍須觀察通膨 、Fed政策及企業獲利表現 。投
資布局建議可以美國投資等級債、美國中短天期公債
及大型AI基礎建設受惠族群為核心配置 ，兼顧收益、
防禦及長期成長潛力。

歷史數據顯示全球股市突破歷史新高後，後續上漲機率高
• 很多人直覺以為見高就是頂，但過去20年的統計資料顯示，全球股市在創下新高後，多數時間選擇繼續向上走。
• 勝率顯著偏高： 全球股市 (MSCI全球指數 )創新高後持有一年 ，獲得正報酬的機率高達 62%，三個月後的正報酬機率更達
71%。美股與台股表現最亮眼，標普500指數創高後一年上漲機率為76%，那斯達克為79%；而台灣股市表現更為驚人，創
高後三個月內上漲的機率高達82%。
• 下檔拉回風險有限：在創高後短期(一個月內)修正幅度其實相當溫和 ，全球多數主要市場的回徹幅度中位數僅 1%～2%左右。
• 突破前高優於連續創新高： 若市場在歷經至少一個月以上的整理後突破前高 ，其未來一年的平均報酬率高達 8.4%，遠高於

連續每天創新高的3.9%。這代表市場經適度整理後的突破，具備更健康的上漲動能。
創新高後的平均遠期報酬率
資料來源：Bloomberg，2026.10.22

市場寬度收窄，由少數巨頭獨撐
• 當前的市場特徵由少數巨頭帶頭衝： 科技股權重較高的指數(如那斯達克、台股、韓股)，長期以來都有少數權重股拉動指數
的現象。自2024年以來，全球13個主要市場中有10個市場在創新高時，參與上漲的股票比例明顯下降 。已開發市場創新高
時高於200日均線的股票比例從過去 75.5%降至65.1%，新興市場更降至 61.3%。這意味著指數看似風光 ，但多數個股並未
同步創高，當漲勢過度集中於少數大型科技巨頭時，整體市場的抗風險能力會相對減弱。
• 小結與投資建議：歷史經驗顯示，創新高本身是強勢市場的特徵，後續繼續走揚的機率遠大於立刻崩盤。然而，鑑於目前市
場寬度收窄、權重高度集中於科技股的現狀 ，盲目追高個股的風險已顯著增加 。投資建議可以利用ETF掌握大盤趨勢，由於

指數上漲主要由龍頭股驅動 ，透過市值型ETF(如追蹤標普500ETF)能直接享受龍頭巨頭帶來的指數創高紅利 ，避免選錯個股
而賺了指數、賠了價差。此外，提防短期股市回檔，可運用定期定額分批布局或等待大盤適度拉回整理再行加碼，降低追高
風險。

法國政局動盪加劇財政壓力，股債同步承壓
• 法國政局動盪加劇財政壓力，股債同步承壓：法國各地抗議活動持續擴大，最初由學生發起，隨後因生活成本高漲而引發其
他群體響應。在財政空間有限、俄烏戰爭與美伊衝突推升能源價格的背景下，政府難以有效緩解民生成本壓力，法國金融市
場因此面臨股債雙跌的局面。
• 公債殖利率攀升，但系統性風險仍相對可控：市場憂心法國政府可能擴大支出以平息抗議，加劇財政可持續性疑慮。法國10
年期公債殖利率一度突破5%，法德利差亦擴大至歐債危機以來高點。不過，歐洲央行系統性壓力綜合指標（CISS）仍處於
歷史相對低檔，遠低於歐元區主權債務危機期間，顯示金融市場尚未出現廣泛的系統性壓力，短期內演變為新一輪主權債務
危機的風險仍相對有限。

日本央行本週料維持利率不變
日本央行利率走勢預估
資料來源： Bloomberg，2026.10.15 資料來源： tradingeconomics，2026.10.21
日本貿易收支
• 日本貿易逆差擴大，進口增幅高於出口：日本2026年9月貿易逆差擴大至5,366億日圓，較去年同期的2,777億日圓增加93%，
高於市場預期的1,810億日圓，連續第五個月呈現貿易赤字。出口年增16.7%至10.97兆日圓，受惠於半導體相關電子零件、
製造設備及汽車出口成長；惟進口年增18.9%至11.51兆日圓，增幅高於出口，主要受到原油、半導體電子零件及液化天然
氣進口增加帶動。其中，原油進口年增51.1%，部分反映日本在荷姆茲海峽局勢緊張下，積極分散能源採購來源。
• 日本央行本週料維持利率不變：日本央行9月將政策利率調升25個基點至1.25%，創31年來新高。考量前幾次升息對經濟的

傳導效果仍待觀察，加上貿易逆差擴大反映進口成本壓力，多數分析師預期日本央行本週將暫停升息，以評估貨幣緊縮對經
濟與通膨的影響。

中國國家統計局公布第三季GDP年增率4.5%，優於市場預期
• 中國國家統計局公布第三季GDP年增率4.5%，優於市場預期：顯示中國經濟在第二季落底後呈現溫和回升 ，從季度表現來看，
一、二、三季增速分別為5.0%、4.3%及4.5%，反映政策支持與外需復甦開始發揮效果 ，全年達成官方4.5%成長目標的機率
明顯提升，第三季經濟改善的主要動能來自出口 ，受惠於全球企業持續擴建 AI基礎設施，及科技產品需求強勁 ，中國高科技
產品出口表現亮眼 ，尤其是積體電路 、伺服器、電腦零組件，及儲存設備等產品增幅顯著 ，高技術製造業維持雙位數成長 。
同時，中國對美國及歐盟出口維持強勁 ，推升貿易順差 走升，為支撐經濟的重要力量 。然而，內需疲弱仍是當前經濟最大隱
憂，房地產調整尚未結束，居民消費與企業投資意願保守，使經濟復甦結構呈現「外熱內冷」的特徵。

• 展望後市：在出口與科技製造業帶動下 ，短期成長可望維持穩定 ，但若內需未能有效改善 ，經濟成長動能恐難全面擴散 ，建
議聚焦在半導體、AI硬體、伺服器供應鏈 ，及高端製造相關產業 ，至於消費、房地產及傳統內需類股 ，仍需觀察後續刺激政
策與消費信心回升情況。
資料來源：開源證券，2026.10.22

彭博預估騰訊、阿里巴巴在中國AI競爭中勝出機率較高
• 彭博預估騰訊、阿里巴巴在中國AI競爭中勝出機率較高：彭博產業研究認為，中國AI產業未來將由大型科技平台主導 ，其中騰
訊、字節跳動與阿里巴巴憑藉龐大用戶基礎 、數據資源、資金實力及完整生態系統 ，在AI競賽中具備明顯優勢 ，這些企業可將
AI廣泛應用於電商、社群、遊戲、金融與雲端業務，提升營運效率並強化競爭門檻，例如：阿里巴巴利用 AI 驅動電商平台上的
個人化產品推薦，而騰訊則運用 AI 優化短影音廣告並強化微信功能。騰訊自 2026 年 7 月起開始將混元 AI 模型整合至其電子
遊戲中，使遊戲角色及助理能更準確地回應玩家 ，然而，AI模型訓練與基礎建設投入龐大 ，獲利能力短期仍受壓抑 ，且須面對
監管、資料隱私及晶片供應等挑戰 。此外，智譜、月之暗面、MiniMax及商湯等AI新創雖具技術創新能力，但缺乏規模效益、

穩定現金流及長期資本支持，未來獲利挑戰較大。
• 投資建議：看好中國AI長期發展者，可優先關注具資金 、算力與應用場景優勢的大型平台企業 ，受惠程度可望高於多數 AI新創
公司。

台灣9月外銷訂單再創新高，台股第四季有望先蹲後跳
• 台灣9月外銷訂單再創新高： 9月外銷訂單1,142.1億美元，連兩個月破千億美元 ，再創單月新高；年增率高達 62.4%，連續
20個月呈雙位數正成長 。經濟部表示，AI強勁需求超乎預期 ，預估今年第四季接單還會優於第三季 。展望10月表現，外銷
訂單將持續亮麗，估落在1,125億至1,159億美元，有望連三個月破千億美元。

布前，市場觀望AI投資是否存在泡沫化風險 。短期來看，台股在季線附近出現支撐 ，不過在11月初的美國期中選舉落幕前 ，

Industry View In-Line
CPO/NPO and micro     VCSEL based   optical solutions are the
focus for networking, while HVDC takes center stage for power.
Higher bandwidth and better power efficiency: In the networking space, the
migration into higher bandwidth continues, as players like Accton and Celestica
showcased their 1.6T network switches. We think a bigger theme is the effort to
improve the power efficiency of current mainstream pluggable optical solutions.

First off, one well-discussed approach is the adoption CPO or NPO, and it seems the
latter is gaining more attention due to the technical challenges in the former. Spec
standardization, such as CPX connectors, could further accelerate the proliferation,
in our view. Secondly, copper could still help, as Credo has its 1.6T AEC cables for
inter-tray transmission while many other vendors (Amphenol, TE Connectivity,
Molex, etc.) have their 448G CPC solutions for in-tray transmission. Lastly, the micro

VCSEL based optical modules from Credo and ALAB represent another power
efficient solution (12W/end at 1,6T), which has reliability comparable to copper
(100M hours of MTBF/MTBI) for a transmission distance of 50-100m. We believe
this micro VCSEL based solution could potentially replace the AEC for shorter
distance transmission when copper hits the physical limit in the medium term and
be integrated with the CPO architecture in the longer term. In sum, we believe the

above-mentioned solutions are likely to co-exist for different use cases.
800VDC for power delivery taking the center stage: While the Vera Rubin NVL72
rack already has the in-rack power shelf reference design (110KW/shelf at 3+1
redundancy), Nvidia unveiled another reference design this time, using one 660KW
800VDC power rack to support two Vera Rubin NVL72 racks. Moreover, we also
saw a few ±400VDC reference designs as well. Our conversations with companies

on the exhibition floor indicate that the HVDC adoption should start from late
2026, but likely at a small scale extending into 2027. The next checkpoint will be
Vera Rubin Ultra racks, where increasing power consumption and more hardware
devices for inter-rack scale-up network might take up more pace on the rack, though
the liquid cooled power shelf  should ease the space budget a bit. We believe both
solutions are progressing and will closely monitor the dynamics.

Stock picks: We believe that Bizlink (3665.TW) should be a beneficiary of the
proliferation of the micro VCSEL based optical solution, as it is the assembly partner
for Credo and potentially others as well. Chroma (2360.TW) should see drivers
from power testing, CPU/GPU/TPU SL T/FT, and photonics testing (pluggable/
CPO)tools. Delta( (2308.TW) and Lite-On (2301.TW) should continue to grow with
the increasing power consumption of AI clusters, but more meaningful growth

and trading securities held by a research analyst account.

CPX and NPX all marching forward
Though CPO remains as the holy grail in the space, its yield issue and lower serviceability
still present challenges to the wider adoption in the near term. We believe that the efforts
will continue, but the industry is simultaneously pursuing other more practical solutions

to sort out the power consumption issue of the existing pluggable transceivers,  including
NPO (near-packaged optics), CPC (co-packaged copper) and NPC (near-packaged copper)
Compared to CPO, NPO compromises on the power consumption, but in exchange it eases
the stringent requirement on alignment and serviceability. Given its better technology
readiness and more mature supply chain, we think it might see good traction in the
coming years.

VCSEL based optical solutions gaining more attention
In addition to CPO/NPO/CPC/NPC designs, we see the micro VCSEL based optical
solutions are gaining more attention too. The value proposition is to leverage the wide
and slow architecture for a relatively short transmission distance of 50-100m. It can
achieve 1-2 pJ/bit of power consumption vs. 5-10pJ/bit for CPO and NPO architecture. The

current generation can enable 32Gbps of bandwidth per lane and the 100-200Gbps of
bandwidth per lane is in the roadmap.

In sum, different solutions of data transmission are emerging, trying to improve the power

Power becoming another bottleneck in the AI deployment
HVDC adoption emerging from Vera Rubin
In the power space, HVDC has been one of the key focuses in the AI cluster, as the rapid
increase in AI rack power density is creating growing challenges for conventional power-
distribution architectures. As rack-level power consumption rising from tens of kilowatts
to several hundreds of KWs, electrical losses, cable size, thermal management
requirements, and overall system complexity increase significantly. Under traditional low-

voltage power architectures, delivering larger amounts of power requires proportionally
higher current, which increases resistive losses and places greater stress on cables,
connectors, busbars, and power-conversion equipment.
At the same time, every power-conversion stage introduces efficiency losses and
generates additional heat. As AI deployments scale into multi-megawatt and eventually
gigawatt-level campuses, even small efficiency improvements can translate into

### Page 10

M
Idea
10
meaningful reductions in operating costs, cooling requirements, and infrastructure
investment. Consequently, the industry is increasingly focused on power architectures
that can reduce conversion stages, lower current levels, and improve overall power-
delivery efficiency.
Against this backdrop, high-voltage direct current (HVDC) is emerging as one of the most
important architectural upgrades for next-generation AI infrastructure. By increasing

distribution voltage, HVDC allows the same amount of power to be delivered at a lower
current, reducing conduction losses and improving system efficiency. Higher-voltage
architectures also improve scalability for ultra-high-density rack deployments by
alleviating some of the physical and thermal constraints associated with conventional
power-distribution systems.
Exhibit 18: Better power efficiency for 800VDC architecture
Source: Delta
Exhibit 19: Better efficiency leads to cost savings

Source: Delta
In a joint keynote speech by Delta and Nvidia, the roadmap illustrates the industry's
gradual migration toward higher-voltage power architectures. Earlier AI server generations
primarily utilized conventional power-delivery schemes centered around rack-level 48V
distribution. As rack power requirements increased with Blackwell-generation systems,
industry participants began evaluating higher-voltage architectures capable of supporting

substantially greater power density. Looking ahead, the industry is increasingly converging
around 800VDC as a potential next-generation standard for large-scale AI deployments,
particularly as rack-level power consumption approaches several hundred kilowatts. The
transition reflects the need to improve power-distribution efficiency while supporting the
continued growth of AI computing infrastructure.
Though we also saw the 800VDC power rack reference design for the Vera Rubin rack in a

1-to-2 configuration (one 660KW power rack supporting two Vera Rubin racks), we think
the adoption of this solution might be relatively low, since the Vera Rubin rack also has a
reference design for the in-rack power shelf design

### Page 11

M
Idea
Morgan Stanley Research 11
Exhibit 20: 800VDC roadmap
Source: Delta
Exhibit 21: 800VDC reference design for VR
Source: Delta
Liquid cooled power shelf on the horizon
The next key check point to watch should be the Vera Rubin Ultra generation, as the
power consumption per rack shall continue to increase and there might be more hardware
devices to enable the inter-rack scale up network, both could take up more space on the
IT rack. That said, the power shelf will shift from air cooling into liquid cooling, which

could narrow the power shelf height by 1U to free up some space as well. Our checks
indicate that both the in-rack power shelf and 800VDC power rack solutions are ongoing
now, and we believe that rack space, power redundancy and system reliability are all the
factors being considered. We'll closely monitor developments on this front.
Exhibit 22: Liquid cooled power shelf
Source: Megmeet
Exhibit 23: Liquid cooled power shelf in rack
Source: Megmeet

Solid state transformers on the Nvidia RVL for the first time
In the MGX ecosystem board, where Nvidia usually list the recommenced vendor lists and
product samples, we saw SST this time. Delta, Heron. DG Matrax, Sungrow, Solaredge,
Megmeet, Luxshare, etc.

### Page 12

M
Idea
12
Exhibit 24: SST vendors on the Nvidia MGX ecosystem board
Source: Morgan Stanley
Looking further ahead, Solid-State Transformers (SSTs) represent a potential next-
generation evolution of power infrastructure. Unlike traditional transformers that operate
primarily as passive devices, SSTs leverage power electronics to provide voltage
conversion, power conditioning, and bidirectional energy management within a single
platform. This enables greater controllability and flexibility while supporting increasingly

digitalized power networks.
Although SSTs remain at an early stage of commercialization and face challenges related
to economics, reliability, and large-scale deployment, the technology aligns well with long-
term data center trends. As AI campuses evolve into utility-scale power consumers
incorporating energy storage, distributed generation, and advanced power management,
SSTs could eventually become a key enabling technology.

### Page 13

M
Idea
Morgan Stanley Research 13
Therefore, while HVDC is expected to drive the next phase of power architecture
upgrades, SSTs represent a longer-term technology roadmap that could further transform
how future data centers distribute and manage electricity.

architecture

2030, as the technology begins to replace a portion of the >$15bn conventional
transformer market, particularly in smaller-power applications. While SST penetration is
expected to remain relatively modest versus the overall transformer market, the
opportunity is significant for relevant players in the longer term.

Lite-On Technology (2301.TW)
Base case: P/E multiple of 17x based on average 2027-28 EPS. We believe our target multiple
is justified by the 47% earnings CAGR we estimate for 2025-28, implying a PEG of 0.4, versus
the stock's 16x P/E and 2% earnings CAGR during 2022-25, which implied a PEG of 7.2.

n PC/NB demand growth

n Downtrend in raw material prices
Risks to Downside
n Macro demand weakness
n Weaker data center/server demand
n PC/NB demand slowdown
n Uptrend in raw material prices

is justified by the 63% earnings CAGR we estimate for 2025-28, implying a PEG of 0.5, versus
the stock's ~23x P/E and 23% earnings CAGR during 2022-25, which implied a PEG of 1.0.

n Faster-than-expected global economic growth
n Falling raw material prices
n Margin improvement
Risks to Downside
n Global economic slowdown
n Ongoing component supply tightness
n Rising raw material prices
n Margin contraction as a result of higher operating expenses or faster ASP erosion

captures Chroma's earnings growth momentum. The stock traded at an average valuation of

~19x P/E during 2014-25 with an operating profit CAGR of 20%, implying a PEG of 1.0. We think
our target multiple of 45x is justified by the 61% operating profit CAGR we project for

STOCK RATINGS
Morgan Stanley uses a relative rating system using terms such as Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated or Underweight (see definitions below). Morgan Stanley does not assign ratings of Buy,
Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

### Page 1

Improving 3Q GPM helped by better mix — Dynapack's preliminary 3Q26 results 
showed margin recovery yet weak PC sales remained the key drag on the earnings. 
3Q26 EPS of NT$2.33 (+72% q/q and +49% y/y) missed our/BBG est by 45%/27%, 
although we believe the earnings shortfall was well anticipated given the 3Q sales 
miss owing to the delayed shipment of one of the BBU products and weaker PC 
demand. GPM improved q/q on a richer product mix, with higher-margin BBU 
making up c.50% of 3Q sales on our estimate, while OPM recovered to 10.7%, 
+4.2ppt q/q and +1.3ppt y/y. Nevertheless, the sales shortfall constrained op 
leverage, leaving margin improvement insufficient to offset weaker sales. 
PSU vendor shortages weigh on Oct; 12kW ramp to drive Nov sales rebound — 
Our industry checks suggest that Dynapack's major PSU vendor has encountered 
component supply shortages (related to power management IC) and therefore 
delayed BBU pull-ins, weighing on shipments of Dynapack's existing 3kW and 5kW 
BBU products in Oct. We therefore expect Oct-26 BBU sales to decline slightly m/m. 
Meanwhile, PC sales should recover sequentially and more than to offset the BBU 
softness, supporting a slight m/m increase in group sales. Looking beyond Oct-26, 
we expect accelerating shipments of the new 12kW BBU series from Nov-26 to drive 
a strong rebound in BBU sales.

Implications — We reiterate our bullish stance on the BBU sector despite near-term
volatility from external supply-chain constraints, which is likely to put pressure on
near-term sentiment. We view the component-driven delays in product pull-ins
primarily as a shipment-timing issue, with underlying demand remaining strong into
2027E. We also see ample opportunity for Dynapack to gain market share from

Panasonic, its major Japanese peer, particularly as power architectures migrate
toward HVDC. This share-gain potential remains intact, reinforcing our confidence
in Dynapack's medium term BBU growth outlook.

Dynapack International (3211.TWO)
19 October 2026 Citi Research
4
Dynapack International
Valuation
We assign a 32x target P/E to our 2027E EPS of NT$25 to derive a target price of NT$800. We value Dynapack based on 2027E
target P/E as we believe 2027E better reflects its earnings power after BBU capacity expansion. Our target P/E of 32x is based
on our forecast EPS CAGR of 66% in 2025-2027E and a PEG of 0.5x, which we benchmark against its major PSU partner, as we

expect Dynapack to grow alongside its partner.
Risks
Our quantitative model assigns a High Risk rating to Dynapack. However, we believe such a rating is not warranted for
Dynapack due to: 1) the company is generating solid revenue and earnings growth over next 3 years; the underlying industry,
BBU for AIDC, also enjoys secular growth; 2) the business model that involves with product customization and strict
safety/reliability requirements would imply relatively benign competition and therefore stable pricing and margins; and 3) the

company has a strong balance sheet and has been in net cash position since 2019.
Key downside risks to our price target/rating are: 1) Slower-than-expected BBU adoption in AIDC, 2) Delay in PSU customer
qualifications, 3) Competition from larger battery or power solution vendors, 4) Safety, certification and reliability issues.
Panasonic Holdings
(6752.T; ¥3957.0; 1; 16 Oct 26; 15:30)
Valuation
To derive our target price, we use a global sum-of-the-parts (SoTP) approach. In the case of Panasonic Holdings, we reference

52 other companies in the Citi coverage universe to set a fair value PER for each business division, which we sum to derive our
overall target price.
Panasonic fair-value PERs for individual business segments range from 8x to 31x, and have a weighted average of 26.2x
(excluding subsidies in the battery business), taking minority interest and equity in earnings into account. We estimate
business-derived equity value of ¥5,000/share. We calculate the present value of the amount we expect Panasonic to receive

in battery subsidies over five years at ¥400/share. Combining these two values, we derive a target price of ¥5,400.
Risks
Risks to our target price include 1) unsuccessful management policies or strategies resulting in weaker-than-expected
business investment returns or sales growth, or conversely producing greater-than-expected results at a relatively early stage,
2) stagnation in the global economy or an upturn in it, and rises or declines in demand in the electrical industry, and 3) forex

trends. If these factors manifest themselves differently than we have anticipated, the share price may vary from our target
price.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do
not consider No View to be a recommendation. For all Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside calls, risk exists that the
catalyst(s) and associated share-price movement will not materialize as expected.

Gigabyte (2376.TW): AI servers to drive 2H26 growth; Capex 
commitments remain strong; Neutral
20 October 2026 | 10:16PM CST
3-month revenues preview: We expect sequential revenue growth in Oct-Dec on a 
YoY basis, driven by rising AI server racks, leading our 4Q26 revenues YoY to 
accelerate to 79% (vs. 60% / 41% YoY in 1Q26 / 2Q26). However, we expect revenue 
decline in Oct-Dec on a MoM basis which we attribute to AI servers model transition 
and the high base in Sep; nevertheless, we continue to expect revenues sequentially 
increase qoq in 4Q26E. We expect the stronger growth in 2H26 to be driven by 
ongoing AI spending, customers’ faster AI deployment, and Gigabyte’s expanding 
customer base and enhancing backlog visibility. Customers’ infrastructure readiness 
and components procurement should continue to improve in 2H26, supporting the 
procurement on AI servers. The company maintains strong capex commitment, and 
we expect 2H26 capex to stay at a similar high level vs. 1H26, leading 2026 capex to 
grow at 148% YoY to NT$2.9bn to capture the strong AI servers demand, and 
customers’ requirements on diversifying production sites (e.g., Gigabyte is across 
Taiwan, US, Europe, and Southeast Asia).

Earnings revision: We factor in Gigabyte’s Sep revenue and raise our 2026E net

### Page 3

Price Target Risks and Methodology - Gigabyte 
We are Neutral rated on Gigabyte with a 12-month target price of NT$492. Our TP is 
based on a 14.2x 2027E P/E which is based on PC/server peers’ EPS growth vs. P/E 
multiple correlation. We see EPS growth as a major factor for the stock’s share 
performance. 
Key upside/downside risks include: 1) stronger-/weaker-than-expected PC market 
recovery, 2) faster-/slower-than-expected gaming PC growth, and 3) 
faster-/slower-than-expected AI server ramp-up.

Source: Company data, Goldman Sachs Research estimates, FactSet. Price as of 20 Oct 2026 close.

on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.

營收走勢圖

研究員/張均壕 JUN_HAO1@uni-psg.com

研究部仍看好時碩未來營運，主要是考量液冷散熱零件業務隨著客
戶訂單逐步放量及出貨規模擴大 ，帶動 2H26 業績大幅提升。 此外 ，
航太事業成長前景仍佳，工業事業亦可望持續增長，明年展望依舊
正向。研究部預估時碩 2026 年 EPS 3.52 元(+162%YoY)，2027 年
EPS 4.63 元(+32%YoY)。以明年獲利為評價基礎，給予 20 倍本益
比，目標價 93 元。

重點摘要：

1. 受惠AI趨勢，液冷散熱訂單將帶動3Q26業績強勁增長： 
時碩3Q26合併營收上升至18.18億元(+24%QoQ，+43%YoY)，主
要是受惠AI資料中心液冷相關零組件的貢獻增加，令7、8、9
月單月營收持續創新高 。考量營收擴大， 規模效應顯現， 預期
時碩3Q26毛利率將提升至 24.5%，營益率提升至 8.8%，預估
3Q26稅後淨利1.38億元(+101%QoQ，+34%YoY)，預估3Q26EPS 
1.26元。

2. 液冷散熱產品進入爬坡期，4Q26營運淡季不淡： 
展望4Q26， 雖然年底通常為時碩汽車、工業業務的淡季， 但受
惠散熱零件仍處在出貨爬坡期，預期季度營收與獲利仍有望
再墊高。研究部預估時碩 4Q26合併營收18.85億元(+4%QoQ，
+55%YoY)，4Q26稅後淨利1.40億元(+1%QoQ，+113%YoY)， 預估
4Q26EPS 1.27元 。 全年度來看 ， 研究部預估時碩2026年合併營
收將提升至65.25億元(+31%YoY)，年度毛利率24.1%，營益率
7.1%，稅後淨利3.87億元(+161%YoY)，預估2026年EPS 3.52
元。

3. 航太業務維持成長動能，積極拓展新商機 
受惠於全球航太產業需求穩健及上游關鍵零組件產能吃緊，
時碩管理層持續看好航太業務前景 ，其中天陽航太營運成長
率預估可達15%～20%。 此外 ， 公司積極擴充泰國航太產能 ， 規
劃建置鋁合金及不鏽鋼生產線，以掌握全球航太供應鏈重組
及非紅供應鏈商機 ， 中長期目標為貢獻約10億元營收 。 除既有
航太業務外，時碩亦積極拓展人形機器人及半導體設備等新
興應用領域。

### Page 2

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  2

在人形機器人方面， 時碩已投入諧波減速器及關節零組件開
發，並與國際及中國大陸主要廠商展開合作。半導體設備方
面，時碩持續開發相關精密零組件，未來將配合泰國新廠布
局 ， 爭取台灣客戶海外工廠的供應商機 。 整體而言 ， 隨著航太
業務穩健成長，以及人形機器人與半導體設備等新領域逐步
拓展 ， 將有助於公司建立更多元的營運成長動能 ， 降低對傳統
汽車產業的依賴。

4. 明年業績續升，預估2027年EPS 4.63元： 
展望2027年 ， 考量日系大廠的液冷散熱訂單將延續至明年 ， 而
且還有美系散熱大廠客戶訂單與磁浮壓縮機關鍵訂單等待發
酵 ， 加上航太業務仍具偏強增速 ， 預期時碩2027年業績將持續
提升。研究部預估時碩2027年合併營收74.20億元(+14%YoY)，
年度毛利率 25.0%， 預估2027年EPS將進一步提升至 4.63元。

附錄 一 ： 評 等 之 標 準 
評等  定義  
強力買進     預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬超過 25%以上  
買進  預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬 10% 以上  
中立  預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬介於 10～-10%  
降低持股  預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬介於 (-10%)以下  
未評等  沒有足夠的基本資料判斷該公司評等

Document 183: [329180] HD현대중공업(329180) 업종 내 여전히 매력적인 선택지HD현대중공업(329180) 업종 내 여전히 매력적인 선택지업종 내 여전히 매력적인 선택지 - 기업

Source PDF: all report pdf/[329180] HD현대중공업(329180) 업종 내 여전히 매력적인 선택지HD현대중공업(329180) 업종 내 여전히 매력적인 선택지업종 내 여전히 매력적인 선택지 - 기업.pdf
Report source: Hankyung
Date: 2026-10-15
Pages: 5
Status: ok

### Page 1

HD현대중공업의 3Q26 연결 실적은 매출액 5조 8,602억원(yoy +33%, qoq -7%), 
영업이익 9,161억원(yoy +64%, qoq -12%, OPM 15.6%)으로 시장 컨센서스를 하회할 
전망이다. 동사의 3Q26 조업일수는 여름휴가, 추석연휴 및 중대재해 등 영향으로 
qoq -21% 감소했으며 환율 역시 qoq -84원 감소한 영향을 감안해 전분기 대비 
실적을 보수적으로 추정했다. 그럼에도 해양 부문 Trion FPU발 C/O에 따른 일회성 
환입이 예상됨에 따라 일부 이익 상쇄가 가능할 것으로 예상된다. 한편, 동사의 
임단협 부결로 당분기 추가적인 인건비 부담은 제한적일 것으로 판단되며 잠수함 
화재 사고 관련 비용 역시 4Q 이후 반영될 전망이다.

동사는 급격히 커져가는 AIDC향 전력수요 및 발전용 엔진 수주에 대응하고자 9/10 
총 4GW 규모의 4행정 중속엔진 캐파 증설을 위한 신규 투자를 공시헸다. 동사의 
기존 DC향 4행정 중속엔진 수주 단가를 고려 시 GW당 약 1조원의 매출 창출이 
가능할 전망이며 2행정 및 선박용 4행정 엔진 대비 높은 수익성이 예상되는 바 
생산이 본격화될 28년부터 동사 실적 성장성의 핵심 동인으로 작용할 전망이다.

동사에 대해 투자의견 BUY를 유지하며 업종 밸류에이션 디레이팅 및 시장 프리미엄 
조정에 따른 Target Multipe 변동으로 목표주가를 70만원으로 하향 조정한다. 상선 
시장 수주 경쟁 심화로 업종 밸류 조정이 이루어지고 있으나 동사는 엔진 기반 장기 
성장성으로 Peer 대비 상대적 매력도 확보 및 주가 방어가 가능하다는 판단이다.

Document 199: AI势头回落并非牛熊分界

Source PDF: all report pdf/AI势头回落并非牛熊分界.pdf
Report source: Unknown
Date: 未知日期
Pages: 3
Status: ok

### Page 1

中信证券表⽰， AI 趋势回落并⾮熊市开端，⾮ AI 可能反⽽会⾯临更为有利的环境，核⼼是
AI 产业在短期内并不像过往的产业趋势对经济体的其他部⻔产⽣⼴泛的拉动作⽤，反⽽对
其他经济部⻔产⽣了不少负外部性（物料和资源价格暴涨、利率飙升、就业挤出）。这也意
味着，过往 “ 产业趋势⾏情结束 = ⽜转熊 ” 的经验可能不再适⽤，⾮ AI 的不少板块早已步⼊下
⾏趋势。
摘要：

AI 趋势回落并⾮熊市开端，⾮ AI 可能反⽽会⾯临更为有利的环境
1 ）产业趋势回落 ≠ ⽜转熊。 A 股在过往存在⽐较⼴泛的 “ 产业趋势回落 = ⽜转熊 ” 的思维，核⼼是
过往的产业趋势（ 2006~2007 年的⼯业与⾦融、 2009 年的地产与基建、 2013~2015 年的移动互
联、 2019~2021 年的能源转型等）对其他经济部⻔带来⼴泛的拉动作⽤。⽽本轮 AI 产业的进
展，⾄少在短期内对其他经济部⻔产⽣了不少挤出效应，包括导致了物料和资源价格暴涨、推
动了债券市场供需紧张和⻓债利率⼤幅飙升、⼀定程度上挤出就业，财富效应的增⻓极为失
衡，市场分化的程度历史罕⻅，甚⾄出现了所谓 “ 硅基世界 ” 和 “ 碳基世界 ” 的对⽴。这也意味着，
过往 “ 产业趋势⾏情结束 = ⽜转熊 ” 的经验可能并不适⽤于这轮 AI 硬件主导的⾏情，⾮ AI 的不少板
块早已步⼊下⾏趋势，⽽ AI 只不过最后 “ 补跌 ” 。
2 ） AI 短期内对其他部⻔产⽣了负外部性，若 AI 势头回落，⼀些⾮ AI 板块反⽽可能有转机。⽬前
已经显性化的负外部性⾄少包含三类：第⼀，电⼦元器件、电⼒等基础⽣活物资的⼤幅涨价，
抬⾼⽣产和⽣活成本，压抑其他领域需求，典型的如消费电⼦；第⼆，算⼒基础设施投建推动
私⼈部⻔巨额债务融资，推升全球⽆⻛险利率，对所有⾼利率敏感性的需求产⽣冲击；第三，
挤出就业，尤其是对于年轻⼈就业的影响，⽐如根据斯坦福与 ADP 就业数据库今年 9 ⽉最新数
据，⾃动化型 AI 使⽤占⽐最⾼职业组的 22~25 岁就业较 2022 年 11 ⽉下降 10.9% ，最低组同期上升
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

7.8% 。这些因素引发了实体经济和市场的剧烈分化，但反过来看，如果 AI 投资的速度在未来有
所放缓，⾮ AI 板块可能也不会⾯临如此负⾯的宏观环境，⽐如⽆⻛险利率出现⼤幅回落。事实
上，从当前的美债定价来看， 10 年期美债利率的⾛向与北美 AI 硬件股的股价⾛势⾼度相关，如
果未来出现持续性的回落，⾄少对海外的⾮ AI 部⻔需求可能产⽣促进。
估值收缩的进程今年已基本完成，⾏情宽度有望逐步拓展
1 ） AI 的⼆阶导修正、⾮ AI 的估值收缩，都有望在今年基本完成。 AI 完成了第⼀波 “ ⼆阶导 ” 修
正。与 2021 年底⾄ 2022 年 4 ⽉底的新能源⾏情调整类似，本轮 AI 同样是在产业景⽓仍在、增速
预期转弱时提前下修估值。从估值收缩的视⻆出发，本轮 AI 硬件的估值压缩幅度已与 2022 年新
能源第⼀轮调整相当。根据 Wind 数据， 7 ⽉ 1 ⽇以来，电⼦与通信板块整体法 PE （ TTM ）从 74.0
倍压缩⾄ 42.4 倍，降幅 42.8% ； 2022 年新能源第⼀轮调整（ 2021 年 12 ⽉ 17 ⽇ -2022 年 4 ⽉ 26

⽇），电⼒设备及新能源板块市值下 PE 从 51.2 倍压缩⾄ 27.8 倍，降幅 45.7% 。站在全球对⽐的视
⻆下， A 股⾮ AI 板块处于全球估值洼地。我们⽤中、美、⽇、韩四个市场的 AI 与⾮ AI ⻰头篮⼦，
以整体法测算 PE （ TTM ）并进⾏跨市场对⽐。截⾄ 10 ⽉ 22 ⽇， A 股是唯⼀⼀个 AI 相对⾮ AI 估值
溢价仍⾼于历史中枢的市场： AI/ ⾮ AI 的 PE 倍数为 4.4 倍，处于 2016 年以来 82% 的分位，美、
⽇、韩的这⼀倍数则分别回落⾄ 1% 、 17% 、 24% 的分位。美、⽇⾮ AI ⾮⾦融⻰头的 PE 分别为
33.5 倍和 23.0 倍，处于 98% 和 95% 的分位； A 股⾮ AI ⾮⾦融⻰头只有 15.6 倍，处于历史 21% 分
位，消费、制造、医药⻰头分别只处于 12% 、 1% 、 3% 的分位。当然，低估值源于疲弱的内需以
及市场对⾮ AI 领域外需的潜在担忧（贸易摩擦、海外⾼利率、⾼油价等），有其合理性。但换个

⻆度看，这种导致低估值的悲观，很⼤程度上源于今年的现实与年初市场期待的巨⼤割裂，投
资者难以预料在 “ ⼗五五 ” 的第⼀年⼴义财政⾚字会意外转为⼤幅负增⻓、市场从美联储降息预期
转为加息预期、 10 年期美债利率创了 2007 年以来新⾼、中欧贸易摩擦突然升温、美伊战争爆发
且持续快⼀年。这种落差，是导致不少⾮ AI 板块在今年业绩增⻓亮眼的情况下估值发⽣剧烈收缩
的原因。当下，⾄少市场已经充分认知到了这些宏观负⾯因素，⽆论是海外利率还是国内政
策，任何边际上超预期的变化可能都有助于改变当下 A 股相对于全球市场更为悲观的估值体系。
2 ） K 型分化逐步收敛，⾏情宽度有望逐步拓展。对于 AI 侧，商业化空间增⻓焦虑还将继续约束
AI 投资单年度的绝对规模体量，同时加深市场对于未来 “ ⼀阶导 ” 拐点的警惕， AI 对于⾮ AI 板块的
虹吸很难再出现，同时考虑到主流机构资⾦仍然超配 TMT 板块，市场反弹过程中的持仓均衡化
过程也将制约 AI 板块的弹性。我们看到，在本周市场⽌跌企稳的过程中，率先反弹的 AI 新技术是

筹码出清相当彻底且机构持仓⽐例低的机器⼈板块，⽽⾮算⼒基建相关的板块。对⾮ AI 板块，当
下⼏乎所有的负⾯因素都已经被市场所认知（即便可能并未完全定价），在经历⼀轮系统性调整
后，低预期本⾝就是有利的条件。以出海板块为例，中欧贸易摩擦已经摆在台⾯上，未来每年
的规则修订谈判和博弈可能是常态化的，企业最终还是会凭借强⼤的竞争优势适应；⼈⺠币在
明年只要升值速度有所放缓，汇兑损失就会同⽐减少，就会带来净利润率的回升；美国中期选
举后⺠主党如果控制国会，中东战争格局可能会有积极变化，所带来的油价影响可能边际上转
弱； AI 投资增速放缓，带来海外⻓端⽆⻛险利率回落，对⾮ AI ⾏业的需求抑制作⽤减弱。由于这
些负⾯因素在今年已经被投资者系统性认知，只要后续有边际上的好转，对⾮ AI 板块本⾝也产⽣
促进。
资⾦⾯的出清需要时间， PB-ROE 代表的稳健回报型策略可能更加占优
资⾦⾯快速恶化的过程⼤概率已接近尾声，在本周 ETF 明显放量的信号下，主动买盘开始谨慎流
⼊市场，融资盘去杠杆的负向循环被阻断。截⾄ 10 ⽉ 21 ⽇，两市融资余额已回落⾄ 2.45 万亿

元，相对年内⾼点累计减少 5600 亿元，降幅 18.6% ，已回到去年 11 ⽉下旬的⽔平，交易层⾯的
降温更为明显，融资买⼊额占两市成交额的⽐例（ MA5 ，下同） 6 ⽉平均为 9.86% ， 7 ⽉降⾄
8.99% ， 9 ⽉为 8.66% ， 10 ⽉以来进⼀步降⾄ 8.01% ， 10 ⽉ 16 ⽇读数 7.84% ，为 2024 年 9 ⽉以来
的最低值，低于 “9.24” ⾏情前约 9% 的⽔平。活跃私募的仓位同样回到低位，对中信证券渠道调
研显⽰，样本活跃私募仓位 8 ⽉ 7 ⽇当周以来持续回落， 10 ⽉ 16 ⽇已降⾄ 67.5% ，为 2024 年 3 ⽉

### Page 4

Monthly Economics
Sector Analysis
Personal Consumption Expenditures (PCE)
• We've lifted our Q3 expectation for real PCE on incoming data and a slightly firmer consumer
backdrop in the wake of recent revisions.
Consumer spending looks firmer in the third quarter, and we now expect real PCE to rise at a 3.4%
annualized clip. This upward revision reflects recent data, with August consumption coming in firm
with real spending rising by the fastest monthly clip in nearly two years, but also reflects a slightly

firmer consumer backdrop in the wake of the BEA's annual revisions released late September. Although
revisions did not materially alter spending, they lifted the level of household income in recent years,
which also boosted the personal saving rate. The trajectory of real income growth remains weak, but
the consumer still looks to be in a slightly stronger financial position than previously believed, which
helps explain recent resilience in spending and suggests consumers may have a bit more runway ahead.

Investment: Equipment, Intellectual Property Products and Inventories
• The AI build out will lead to another surge in business fixed investment spending in Q3, though the
effect on headline growth will be partly offset by stronger imports.
Business fixed investment spending continues to be supported primarily by the high-tech buildout.
We forecast real equipment investment to rise at around a 16% annualized clip in Q3, but part of
this boost to headline GDP growth will be offset by stronger import growth that is facilitating the

build out. Net imports of computers are tracking to surge in the third quarter, which will provide a
sizable lift to information processing equipment investment, or around half of overall equipment.
And while non-defense capital goods shipments (including aircraft) slipped in August, the near-term
growth trajectory remains supportive of a pickup in equipment. The rebuild in inventories we have
been anticipating is also tracking to be a bit stronger as businesses replenish drawn down product.

Investment: Residential
• The abrupt rise in mortgage rates is likely to dampen residential investment over the next few
quarters. Residential spending should find support next year as longer-term interest rates
moderate somewhat.
We are unlikely to see another positive residential investment reading in Q3. Rising mortgage rates

have sent a chill over builder outlooks, pushing sales expectations to their weakest level in more than
three years. While builders have responded with deeper price cuts, affordability pressures continue
to weigh on single-family sales and construction outlays. Apartment construction is simultaneously
soft amid rising financing costs and modest rent growth, and home improvement spending remains
constrained by high materials costs and interest rates. While residential investment is likely to remain

4 | Economics

### Page 5

Economic Outlook: October 2026 Economics
a drag this year, we expect a slight moderation in longer-term interest rates in 2027, which would
provide a more supportive backdrop.
Investment: Nonresidential Structures
• We have not made material changes to our nonresidential structures forecast. Despite elevated
interest rates, data center and related spending should continue to support structures investment
through our forecast horizon.
Nonresidential structures spending has accelerated sharply, overwhelmingly driven by data centers

and electric power facilities. As data center construction expands geographically and demand rises
for semiconductors, high-tech capex has also started to generate positive spillovers for warehousing
and manufacturing construction. This buildout, which appears relatively insensitive to interest rates,
should be enough to sustain solid growth in structures investment even as spending across most
other categories remains essentially flat. Domestic energy is another green shoot, with elevated prices

supporting ongoing growth in active rotary rigs.

Net Exports & Trade
• An expected drag from trade in Q3 got larger in light of a near-record trade deficit in August, net
exports is now expected to lop off three full percentage points from third quarter growth.

Exports increased in August for the first time in four months, but the increase was more than offset by
an even larger increase in imports. The upshot was a widening in the trade deficit to a near-record gap
of more than $105 billion. Going into the report, we had a 1.9 percentage point drag from net exports
penciled in for Q3 GDP; we revised that to a full 3.0 percentage points drag in light of the record gap.
Even a modest narrowing in the final months of the year would likely mean a lift to headline GDP in

Q4, but the real story here is that trade will remain a headwind throughout the forecast period owing
largely to surging imports of tech-related products amid the AI boom.
Labor Market
• The jobs market remains stable. We continue to expect the unemployment rate to end the year at
4.2% and hold near that level in 2027.
We modestly revised down our near-term payroll growth forecast following the September jobs
report, but otherwise we have left our labor market outlook unchanged. Measures of labor demand

growth continue to point to a stable labor market. Job openings have inched higher on a year-ago
basis but remain broadly steady while initial jobless claims, WARN notices and Challenger job cut
announcements continue to signal low layoffs. Labor force participation appears to be improving after
its sharp pullback in the first half of the year. Looking ahead, we expect payroll growth to average
around 60K per month in 2027, roughly in line with our estimate of breakeven job growth. That should

Economics | 5

### Page 6

Monthly Economics
keep the unemployment rate near 4.2% in 2027 and leave labor market conditions broadly consistent
with "full employment." Notably, our expectation for labor cost growth of ~3.4% is the reason we
continue to reiterate that labor cost growth is not a contributor to the excess inflation seen in the
data.
Inflation
• We expect the core PCE deflator to rise 3.2% and 2.6% Q4/Q4 in 2026 and 2027, respectively.
Headline inflation remains heavily influenced by developments in the Middle East, with higher oil

prices lifting gasoline and diesel prices. Food inflation has been tame this year but looks poised to
strengthen as higher transportation and commodity prices work their way through supply chains.
Beyond food and energy, price pressures remain concentrated in a handful of categories, most notably
airfares and AI-related tech goods. Core goods inflation is showing signs of stabilizing, as waning tariff-
related price pressures have been offset by resilient demand tied to the AI buildout. We expect some

improvement here in 2027. Still-low shelter inflation and cooler price growth for airfares and health
care should help bring about some moderation in core services inflation next year. We expect the core
PCE deflator to rise 3.2% on a Q4-over-Q4 basis in 2026 before slowing to 2.6% in 2027.

Fiscal Policy
• Fiscal year (FY) 2026 ended on September 30, and the federal government tallied a $2.0 trillion
budget deficit (6.5% of GDP). We project the deficit to be unchanged in dollar terms in FY 2027

and a bit smaller as a share of GDP (~6%).
• Another continuing resolution to extend government funding past the December 11 deadline
seems like the most likely outcome at this time for the FY 2027 budget. It is possible Republicans
use the lame duck session of Congress to increase defense spending and raise the debt ceiling.
Looking ahead to the lame duck session of Congress that will follow the midterm elections, Congress
needs to pass a bill that extends government funding past December 11 to avert a government

shutdown. Another continuing resolution that punts the issue into Q1 seems like the most likely
outcome. Perhaps more interesting will be whether Republicans attempt to use budget reconciliation
to pass one more partisan piece of legislation. If they do, we suspect the two things at the top the
agenda will be more funding for national defense and immigration enforcement priorities as well as a
debt ceiling increase. For the latter, raising it in the lame duck would remove some leverage Democrats

could have in 2027 if they recapture one or both chambers. It is still very early, but we estimate the
debt ceiling "X date" by which Congress must act or face default is sometime in late Q3/early Q4 of
next year.

Economic Outlook: October 2026 Economics
Monetary Policy & Interest Rates
• Our base case forecast is for the FOMC to hike the fed funds rate by 25 bps in December and
then keep the policy rate unchanged in 2027 at 4.00%-4.25%, with risks skewed toward additional
tightening.
As we go to print, the FOMC seems more likely than not to keep the fed funds rate unchanged in
October. But given the rationale under which the Fed started this tightening cycle and the forcefulness

with which Warsh has driven home his displeasure with elevated inflation, it seems entirely plausible
to us that October will be a live meeting. At a minimum, the recent inflation data seem to make a hike
in December a lock. Past December, the outlook will be highly dependent on future inflation data. Our
expectation is that core inflation will cool gradually in 2027, and we still do not see signs of demand-
side inflation in the data. But hiking cycles have an inertial component to them, and the recent inflation

data reset the clock to zero on the Fed seeing several good inflation prints in a row. This creates upside
risk to our base case for the fed funds rate.

International Developments
• Global growth remains resilient, leaving our 2026 and 2027 forecasts unchanged. Our inflation
forecasts are also unchanged, although risks of delayed price pass-through could prolong inflation
pressures into 2027.

• Monetary policy continues to lean toward tightening across advanced economies, while emerging
market central banks are increasingly cautious, either holding rates or easing more gradually as
inflation pressures rebuild.
The balance of risks to global inflation remains tilted to the upside, complicating the monetary policy
outlook even as growth has proven more resilient than expected. Elevated energy prices, particularly
for refined petroleum products, persistent geopolitical uncertainty and El Niño-related food price

risks could prolong inflationary pressures into 2027. While second-round effects still remain relatively
contained, repeated supply shocks are testing central banks’ willingness to look through the temporary
price increases, particularly where inflation expectations remain elevated, even as higher government
bond yields are already contributing to tighter financial conditions.
Across advanced economies, central banks face increasingly difficult trade-offs as persistent inflation

pressures limit the scope for policy accommodation. The Eurozone remains particularly vulnerable
to energy supply disruptions, with elevated natural gas prices and low storage levels adding to
inflation risks. We continue to expect a 25 bps European Central Bank hike in December, followed by a
prolonged hold through 2027. Fiscal concerns are also adding to pressure, particularly in France, where
the selloff in government debt has pushed yields higher. In the U.K., resilient activity and renewed

Economics | 7

### Page 8

Monthly Economics
inflation pressures similarly underpin our expectation for a Bank of England hike in November,
followed by a hold through 2027.
Elsewhere, policy paths are becoming more differentiated. After four hikes this year brought the Cash
Rate to 4.60%, we expect the Reserve Bank of Australia to remain on hold, although renewed inflation
pressures or stronger-than-expected activity could prompt further tightening. We also still expect
the Bank of Canada to hold at 2.25%, with subdued growth weighing against energy-driven inflation

risks, although incoming inflation data over the coming weeks will be critical in determining whether
persistent price pressures warrant tightening monetary policy perhaps as early as the December
meeting. Japan remains an exception to the broader hold outlook: while the yen's recovery provides
some relief from imported inflation, elevated energy prices and import dependence continue to pose
upside risks. We expect the Bank of Japan to hike again by year-end and now expect an additional 25

bps hike in Q3-2027, bringing the terminal rate to 1.75%.
The emerging market story has increasingly shifted from easing toward patience or selective
tightening. In Asia, China's weak domestic demand and persistent property-sector strains continue to
warrant policy support, with AI-related exports providing some offset. We expect a more gradual pace
of Reserve Requirement Ratio cuts alongside targeted monetary and fiscal measures. India faces the

opposite challenge, with elevated food and energy prices and rupee depreciation strengthening the
case for further Reserve Bank of India tightening. In Latin America, peso depreciation and elevated
energy costs pose upside inflation risks for Mexico, although limited exchange-rate pass-through
should allow Banxico to remain on hold while policymakers monitor underlying inflation and inflation
expectations. In Brazil, election-related uncertainty and renewed inflation pressures are likely to delay

further easing, with cuts expected to resume in Q1-2027 once policymakers see more convincing
evidence of disinflation.

SK 海⼒⼠据报计划将出售铠侠股权所获约 20 万亿韩元净利中的⼋成，以 16 万亿韩元（约合
121 亿美元）特别股利形式回馈股东，并拟于 10 ⽉ 28 ⽇三季报发布时正式宣布。
摘要：
SK 海⼒⼠据报将把出售铠侠股权所获巨额收益的⼤部分，以特别股利形式直接返还股东。
据韩国《东亚⽇报》援引投资银⾏业知情⼈⼠报道， SK 海⼒⼠计划派发约 16 万亿韩元（约 121
亿美元）特别股利，资⾦来源为出售⽇本存储芯⽚制造商铠侠（ Kioxia ）股权所获收益。
该计划预计将于 10 ⽉ 28 ⽇（周三） SK 海⼒⼠公布第三季财报时正式对外宣布。
据估计， SK 海⼒⼠在第⼀季及第⼆季陆续出售铠侠持股，扣除原始投资成本后，合计净利约达
20 万亿韩元（约合  151 亿美元）。

此次拟派发的 16 万亿韩元特别股利，约相当于上述净利的⼋成。
2018 年⻉恩资本牵头收购东芝存储 ( 即后来的铠侠 ) 时， SK 海⼒⼠通过两个 SPC( 特殊⽬的公司，
常⽤来隔离债务和法律⻛险 ) 分别买⼊了铠侠的股票和可转债。随着今年⻉恩卖出最后⼀批铠侠
股份，海⼒⼠也逐渐清仓了该公司的股票。

### Page 3

Helen Belopolsky, Miha Hribernik | This Month in Geopolitics | October 2026
Deutsche Bank
Research Institute
Diplomacy appears firmly back in play. The past month saw continued fighting and renewed diplomacy.
Reporting suggests that the US is prepared to extend troop deployments in the Middle East to 2027. Iran
targeted both US forces in the region and some tankers moving through the Strait, but has been unable to
prevent a significant rebound in crude flows through Hormuz. On 22 SEP, talks were mediated by Qatar,

Pakistan and Egypt, and on 24 SEP, Iran presented the US with a seven-day roadmap to a ceasefire leading
to a phased reopening of the Strait of Hormuz and a return to nuclear negotiations, conditional on a US
lifting of its blockade of Iran, sanctions relief and broader cessation of hostilities. The Trump
Administration rejected the proposal, but negotiations continue.
To watch will be whether October is the month the war transitions into a negotiated settlement, which

may hinge on whether Iran is willing to make some concessions on sanctions relief and the nuclear
question, and whether Trump is keen to demonstrate that the war has produced a political and economic
result in the final weeks before the 3 NOV midterm elections. It is plausible that the current diplomatic
channel could produce a temporary de-escalation mechanism as long as there is pathway around the
broader nuclear issue.
Both sides will reconvene for trade talks in Beijing on 8-9 October, which the EU views as a deadline for

“tangible results”. Brussels remains focused on its rising trade deficit with China – which has reached a
record €1 billion per day – and the impact of Chinese goods on European industry. The bloc has vowed to
take measures if talks fail.
While both sides could achieve progress in talks, a lasting breakthrough appears improbable, setting the
stage for more trade tensions into 2027. New EU trade investigations, curbs on Chinese imports, and EU-
level instruments intended to address alleged Chinese overcapacity, are possible outcomes, to which

Beijing has stated it would respond.
China-EU trade talks could also impact China-US relations. Should talks with Brussels fall short of averting
increased European trade pressure, we assess that Beijing may further prioritise near-term US stability. See
Rules of Engagement: Mapping the Next Phase of China-US Ties for more.

### Page 4

Helen Belopolsky, Miha Hribernik | This Month in Geopolitics | October 2026
Deutsche Bank
Research Institute
Latvia will elect all 100 members of the 15th Saeima. Latvia, which borders Russia and Belarus, has been
among the most vocal supporters of Ukraine. The next government will set the country’s course on
defence spending, Ukraine aid, sanctions enforcement and border security. The field is highly fragmented
with the governing parties lacking a majority in the polls. The fragmentation matters as a weakened and

multi-party scramble could mean a slower, more unstable coalition.
Brazil’s first electoral round is on 4 OCT, with a runoff on 25 OCT. This is a critical election as it will
decide whether Latin America’s biggest economy continues President Lula’s state-led social spending or
pivots to a more fiscally-orthodox agenda under Bolsonaro. Polls have gone from a Lula lead in the spring
to a statistical dead heat between Lula and Bolsonaro. A Brazilian Supreme Court crisis – which has

implicated Supreme Court Justices and politicians on both sides – is weighing on the race. Congressional
and gubernatorial races will also impact the governability of the country.
This election will also shape whether the country hedges between Washington and Beijing under Lula or
tilts toward closer US trade and security alignment under Bolsonaro with potential implications for
tariffs, BRICS+ cohesion, and Latin America’s broader positioning between the two powers.

The meetings will take place in Bangkok, bringing finance ministers, central bankers, policy-makers and
business leaders together. The agenda is expected to centre on global growth, financial stability, debt,
trade and investment, development finance and the impact of AI on jobs and productivity, with particular
attention given to mobilizing private capital. The key watchpoint will be the outlook for global growth
and inflation, debt vulnerabilities, the future of the international financial architecture and how policy-

makers respond to increasing fragmentation in trade and capital flows.

### Page 5

Helen Belopolsky, Miha Hribernik | This Month in Geopolitics | October 2026
Deutsche Bank
Research Institute
Leaders are expected to focus heavily on Ukraine, European security and defence, competitiveness and
the EU’s economic position in a more contested global environment. They will also take forward
negotiations on the 2028-2034 EU long-term budget. They set themselves a soft deadline in June to get
the budget broadly locked down because missing it pushes ratification into 2027 and risks a funding gap

for beneficiaries from JAN 2028. To watch will be a genuine breakthrough on the multiannual framework
or more likely what “substantial” progress may look like. Ukraine funding is the other pressure point. This
meeting will be important for understanding the direction of European defence spending and industrial
policy, the next phase of budget negotiations and the EU response to growing external economic
pressure and strategic dependencies.
The Canadian province of Alberta will hold a referendum, which includes a question about the province’s

future within Canada. This is not an independence vote itself but asking voters to choose between
remaining a Canadian province and asking the Alberta government to begin the constitutional process
required for a future binding referendum on separation. Current polling suggests “remain” is comfortably
ahead. The significance is around potential renewed uncertainty around Canada’s federal cohesion,
energy policy, fiscal transfers and Alberta's relationship with Ottawa and Washington. Even a modest

agreement to begin a process towards secession would likely keep alive equalization grievances as a
structural fault line in Canadian federalism feeding into political risk.
.
The event’s theme focuses on “Advancing Alliances in a New Era.” It will tackle how governments and
businesses respond to a world where geopolitics, economic security, supply chains and technology are
increasingly intertwined. There is likely to be a strong focus on the future of the transatlantic

relationship, including how Europe adapts to a more demanding security environment. There will also be
discussions of tariffs, supply chain resilience, critical capabilities and the weaponization of economic
interdependence. It will align with Chancellor Merz’s efforts to ensure Europe actively shapes outcomes
around strategic autonomy and reducing China exposure in manufacturing.

### Page 6

Helen Belopolsky, Miha Hribernik | This Month in Geopolitics | October 2026
Deutsche Bank
Research Institute
The second summit will take place in Saudi Arabi, bringing EU and Gulf leaders together. The agenda is
expected to focus on regional security and the Middle East, energy and economic security, trade and
investment, infrastructure, technology and broader strategic cooperation. The security dimension will be
particularly salient given the current crisis in the region. Economically, the relationship is substantial: EU-

GCC trade reached €165.6bn in 2025.
To watch will be movement on the long-stalled EU-GCC free trade talks and visa-waiver requests from
Qatar and Saudi Arabia in addition to the extent of the focus on Gaza reconstruction, Iran and regional
security get relative to the economic agenda.
GCC states have consistently used these summits to press for clearer EU positioning on Middle East
conflicts in exchange for economic cooperation. The summit comes amid at a critical juncture where

Europe is recalibrating its external partnerships.
The Central Committee of the Communist Party of China will hold its Fifth Plenary Session in Beijing.
This will be one of the most important events on the country’s political calendar ahead of the 21st Party
Congress in late 2027.
The Plenum will focus on Party governance, and investors will scrutinise signals regarding leadership
changes and personnel reshuffles, institutional reforms, and political priorities for the next Congress.

While fifth plenums historically focus on economic planning, the development guidelines for the 15th
Five-Year Plan (2026–2030) were already adopted during the Fourth Plenum in OCT 2025, allowing the
upcoming session to focus on personnel and governance matters.

Elections will be held for the 26th Knesset, the first since the October 7th attacks and wars in Gaza,
Lebanon and Iran. This is the first Knesset since 1988 to actually complete a full four-year term without

Naftali Bennett’s new “Together” party is polling as the main rival bloc, alongside Gadi Eisenkot’s Yashar,

Benny Gantz’s Blue and White, and the further right Ben Gvir and Smotrich factions.
Israel’s proportional system means the election is effectively about coalition formation. Key faultlines to

in Gaza factor into campaign messaging, and whether a Netanyahu-led bloc can reassemble a working

outcome will shape Israel’s posture on critical regional issues, including Gaza reconstruction, Iran policy
and West Bank settlement activity.
This summit is shaping up as a particularly important juncture against a more ambitious push to redefine

become the EU’s first “associate member”.

digital cooperation and supply-chain resilience, building on the EU-Canada security and Defense
Partnership in the EU’s SAFE defense initiative. The summit will be watched for concrete progress on a
digital trade agreement and broader economic cooperation.

產業更新 – 半導體產業 
400G/lane 驅動下一代光調變與封裝升級 
What’s New - TFLN 邁入量產並開啟 400G/lane 封裝新週期 
TFLN 由技術驗證跨入商業量產階段，HyperLight、聯電(2303 TT)及聯穎光電共同擴大 TFLN 量
產平台，其中聯穎 6 吋產線已完成客戶驗證，聯電則導入 8 吋。產品端亦由 200G/lane 快速往
400G/lane 升級。相較矽基調變器在高速化後面臨頻寬、插入損耗及驅動電壓間的取捨，TFLN
利用 Pockels Effect具備高頻寬、低光損及低驅動電壓優勢，並有望降低獨立射頻Driver 需求。
隨 TFLN 頻寬提升至 100~150GHz 以上，封裝介面逐漸成為新瓶頸，開始導入 Flip-Chip 及凸
塊接合以縮短 Driver-to-Modulator 電性路徑，Gold Bump 亦有望受惠異質整合需求增加。 
Trends/Outlook - 高速光互連進入材料分工並帶動調變器升級 
展望 27~30 年，我們認為 TFLN 不會全面取代矽光子，而將優先切入對頻寬、功耗及訊號品質
要求較高的高速光調變器，形成 Si/SiN 負責被動光路、TFLN 負責主動調變、III-V 負責雷射及
CMOS 負責訊號處理的異質整合架構。26~27 年 1.6T/200G per lane 仍以矽光子為主流，
27~29 年進入 3.2T/400G per lane 後，TFLN 憑藉 130~150GHz 以上頻寬、低 Vπ 及低插入
損耗優勢有望加速滲透。隨產業由 6 吋往 8 吋 TFLN 量產發展，競爭焦點將逐步轉向成本、良
率及高速封裝能力 ， 並帶動Flip-Chip、RDL、Cu Pillar 及 Gold Bump 等晶圓級互連需求提升 。

Investment Summary 
1. TFLN 產業正處於由高性能元件走向可量產平台的關鍵轉折期，投資主軸可分為前段晶圓製
造與後段高速互連。聯電(2303 TT) 已正式加入 HyperLight 與聯穎的 TFLN 量產體系，負責將
既有 6 吋平台進一步擴展至 8 吋，同時公司本身亦具備 12 吋矽光子量產與 400G/lane 純矽平
台布局，因此並非單壓 TFLN 取代 Silicon，而是同時掌握 Si 與 TFLN 兩條技術路線，可在
200G/lane 維持矽光子量產優勢，並於 400G/lane 以上參與 TFLN 及 Si/TFLN 異質整合。 
2. 頎邦(6147 TT)則位於 TFLN 後段高速互連環節 ， 公司目前已透過矽光子切入PD、TIA、Driver
及 Modulator，並具備 Gold Bump 、Cu Pillar、RDL、晶圓測試及切割等完整後段能力。隨
TFLN 由 6 吋往 8 吋量產擴張 ， 以及400G/lane 以上產品提高 Driver-to-Modulator Flip-Chip
滲透率，頎邦既有晶圓級 Bumping 與互連平台有望直接受惠。

2 
TFLN 邁入量產階段並開啟 400G/lane 高速封裝新週期 
TFLN(Thin-Film Lithium Niobate， 薄膜鈮酸鋰)產業於 25~26年由技術驗證跨入商業量產階段 ，
產業發展重心亦由過去追求單一元件的超高頻寬，逐步轉向 8 吋晶圓製造、客戶認證、模組整合
及高速封裝。26 年 HyperLight、聯電(2303 TT)及聯穎光電共同擴大 TFLN 量產平台，其中聯穎
既有 6 吋產線已完成客戶驗證，聯電則進一步導入8 吋製造，代表TFLN 開始進入成熟晶圓代工
體系 ， 補足過去在晶圓均勻性 、 製程穩定度 、 良率及量產規模上的主要瓶頸 。 產品端亦快速升級 ，
HyperLight 目前 TFLN 發射光子晶片電光頻寬已達 130~150GHz 以上 ， 部分低壓版本差動驅動
電壓可低至約 0.6Vpp；26 年 OFC 更已展示 210GBaud、420Gbps/lane PAM4 的 TFLN 發射
光學次模組，產品藍圖則進一步指向448Gbps/lane，顯示 TFLN 已由 1.6T/200G per lane 逐步
進入 3.2T/400G per lane 世代。 
值得注意的是 ，400G/lane 並非矽光子的技術終點 ， 目前純矽MZM 亦已完成 400Gbps/lane 展
示，聯電也同步與 SILITH 開發 400G/lane 純矽光子平台。因此 TFLN 真正的優勢並非矽無法做
到 400G，而是隨傳輸速率提高，矽基調變器使用自由載子電漿色散效應，在頻寬、插入損耗、
驅動電壓及調變效率之間的取捨逐漸放大；相較之下，TFLN 利用 Pockels Effect，可同時維持較
高頻寬 、 較低光損及較低驅動電壓 ， 使其優勢逐漸由元件性能延伸至整體光模組功耗 。HyperLight
於 26 年展示的 1.6T DR8 參考模組整體功耗約 20W，低 Vπ特性使 TFLN 具備降低甚至取消獨
立射頻 Driver 的潛力 ， 加上較低插入損耗可減少雷射功率需求 ， 使TFLN 開始由高速光調變材料
轉變為改善整體功耗與物料成本的系統級方案。

圖一. Overall design of the broadband thin-film lithium niobate (TFLN)

資料來源: Photonics 2025, 12(5), 452、國泰證期研究部整理

另一方面，隨 TFLN 本體電光頻寬提升至 100~150GHz 以上 ， 封裝介面逐漸成為新的性能瓶頸 。
傳統 Driver 與 Modulator 透過印刷電路板及 Wire Bond 傳輸高速訊號，在 100GHz 以上容易
受到寄生電感、寄生電容及阻抗不連續影響，因此產業開始導入 Flip-Chip 與凸塊接合，縮短
Driver-to-Modulator 的高頻電性路徑。26 年已有多通道 TFLN Flip-Chip 平台完成 13 條高速
通道與 32 條低速通道整合，整體接合良率達 94%，顯示 TFLN 的商業化重點正由前段元件性能
進一步延伸至 2.5D/3D 封裝與晶圓級互連 。 不過TFLN 高速化並不等同於 Gold Bump 需求等比
例增加，實際接合材料仍可能採用 Gold Bump、Cu Pillar、Solder 等不同方案；因此真正受惠

### Page 3

投資 顧問 
產業報告

2026/10/21

3 
的是高速 Bumping 及晶圓級互連市場，而金凸塊則憑藉抗氧化、高延展性、低溫熱壓接合及異
質材料應力吸收能力，在 TFLN、Si 與 InP 等不同材料整合中具備潛在優勢。

高速光互連進入材料分工時代並帶動 TFLN 調變器升級  
展望 27~30 年，我們認為 TFLN 最可能的發展路徑並非全面取代矽光子，而是優先取代矽光子
平台中對速度 、 功耗及訊號品質最敏感的高速光調變器功能 ， 並逐步形成Si/SiN 負責被動光路 、
TFLN 負責高速主動調變、III-V 負責雷射、CMOS 負責電子訊號處理的異質整合架構。矽光子仍
具備成熟 12 吋晶圓製造、生態系、設計套件、低成本被動光波導及 Ge 光偵測器整合等優勢，
因此在 200G/lane 及部分 400G/lane 市場仍將保持主流 ； 但當產品進一步進入400G/lane 甚至
更高速世代，約 200GBaud 級訊號對頻寬、 Driver、光損及功耗要求快速提高， TFLN 的
130~150GHz 以上頻寬、低 Vπ 及低插入損耗將逐漸轉化為更具體的系統成本優勢。我們因此
認為 400G/lane 不是矽光子的終點，但可能是 TFLN 由高性能利基技術進入資料中心大量市場
的重要轉折點。

表 1. TFLN vs Silicon MZM 比較 
資料來源：國泰證期研究部整理 
從時間軸來看，26~27 年的 1.6T/200G per lane 預期仍以矽光子為主流，TFLN 主要透過低功
耗、降低 Driver 需求及改善光損切入部分高階產品；27~29 年進入 3.2T/400G per lane後，隨
高頻訊號餘裕與整體功耗預算進一步收窄，TFLN 的性能優勢有望加速轉化為商業價值。更長期
而言，我們預期市場將朝 TFLN-on-Si、TFLN-on-SiN 及其他異質整合平台發展，而不是單一材
料完全取代另一材料。目前 200mm TFLN/SiN 異質整合平台及 TFLN 整合至矽光子後段製程的
研究均已出現，顯示 TFLN 更可能以 Chiplet 或主動式光調變層的形式加入既有矽光子生態系。

投資 顧問 
產業報告

2026/10/21

4 
TFLN 量產規模亦將成為下一階段競爭重點 。 過去產業瓶頸主要在LNOI 晶圓供應 、 薄膜均勻性 、
蝕刻品質與良率 ， 目前HyperLight/聯穎/聯電 、Lightium 及其他供應商均逐步往 8 吋平台發展 ，
顯示 200mm TFLN 已成產業共同方向。當 8 吋晶圓供應與量產良率成熟後，競爭焦點將由是否
能生產轉向成本能否下降以及封裝是否能支援 100GHz 以上高頻介面 。 這將同步提高Flip-Chip、
RDL、Cu Pillar 及 Gold Bump 等晶圓級互連的重要性，尤其當 Driver-to-Modulator 電性路徑
必須由數百微米至毫米級 Wire Bond 縮短至數十微米時，封裝將從過去的後段支援製程，轉變
為決定 TFLN 能否實現 400G/lane 以上性能的核心環節。 
400G/lane 推動 TFLN 商業化 ， 聯電與頎邦分掌前後段關鍵環節 
我們認為 TFLN 產業目前正處於由高性能元件進入可量產平台的關鍵轉折期，投資主軸可分為前
段晶圓製造與後段高速互連兩個方向。聯電(2303 TT)已正式加入 HyperLight 與聯穎的 TFLN 量
產體系，負責將平台由 6 吋進一步擴展至 8 吋，同時公司本身亦具備 12 吋矽光子量產與
400G/lane 純矽平台布局。因此聯電並非只壓 TFLN 取代 Silicon，而是同時掌握 Si 與 TFLN 兩
條技術路線，可在 200G/lane 以矽光子取得量產規模、400G/lane 提供 Pure-Si 與 TFLN 雙平
台，並在更高速世代參與Si/TFLN 異質整合。我們認為其投資價值主要來自特殊製程光子晶圓代
工平台的擴張，而非單一 TFLN 產品貢獻。 
據供應鏈了解，目前美系客戶對 TFLN 的導入興趣明顯升溫，TFLN 需求正逐步擴散至更多光通
訊業者。隨 1.6T 產品量產及 3.2T/400G per lane 規格持續推進，客戶對低功耗、高頻寬及低驅
動電壓光調變器的需求提高，使 TFLN 開始成為美系高速光通訊廠商的重要技術評估方向。我們
認為，若後續更多美系客戶由送樣與驗證進一步轉入量產 將加速 8 吋 TFLN 晶圓及相關後段
Bumping 需求放量。 
頎邦(6147 TT)的受惠邏輯則位於 TFLN 後段製程，公司目前已透過矽光子切入 PD、TIA、Driver
及 Modulator，並具備 Gold Bump、Cu/Ni/Au、Cu Pillar、RDL、晶圓測試、研磨及切割等完
整後段能力，同時可支援6 吋與 8 吋晶圓製程。我們預期聯電TFLN 相關後段製程將主要交由頎
邦承接 ， 並預計自26 年年底開始貢獻營收 ， 因此無論既有6 吋 TFLN 需求或後續 8 吋平台放量 ，
頎邦皆有望直接受惠晶圓級 Bumping、RDL 及相關後段製程需求成長。隨400G/lane 以上產品
逐步提高 Driver-to-Modulator Flip-Chip 滲透率，頎邦既有高速 Bumping 與晶圓級互連能力
的重要性亦將進一步提升。 
整體而言，我們對 TFLN 產業的核心判斷是 400G/lane 不代表 Silicon 退出，而是 TFLN 的高頻
寬、低驅動電壓與低光損開始具備足以抵銷較高材料與製造成本的系統級價值。 若未來 DSP 直
接驅動、8 吋 TFLN 量產及 Driver-to-Modulator Flip-Chip 三項技術同步成熟，TFLN 有望優先
取得高速光調變器市場 ， 而Si/SiN 仍負責被動光路與大規模整合 。 聯電為目前較明確的前段TFLN
量產受惠者，頎邦則提供後續高速晶圓級互連的選擇權；後續最重要的觀察指標包括 8 吋 TFLN
良率與成本、400G/lane 產品實際量產時間、Driverless 架構滲透率，以及 TFLN 封裝由 Wire 
Bond 轉向 Flip-Chip 的速度。這些因素將分別決定 TFLN 產業的可服務市場，以及聯電與頎邦
最終能取得的實際營收與獲利貢獻。

紀彥呈 / 資訊軟體、半導體  
+886-2-2326-9899#1940 / ianchi@cathayfut.com.tw

台積電 2330 TT  ( 電子–半導體 ) 
積厚成勢，晶耀巔峰 
投資與評價 
1. 台積電 3Q26 受惠 AI GPU、ASIC、CPU 及網通晶片需求強勁，HPC 營收
季增 22%，N2 營收占比提升至 8%，營運創新高；4Q26 隨 N2 於新竹及
高雄持續快速放量，N3 維持高稼動率，營運續創歷史新高。 
2. 台積電 26 年受惠 AI/HPC 需求強勁，N3 維持高稼動 、N2 加速放量，A16
於 2H26 量產，CoWoS 產能持續擴充，預估 EPS 110.83 元；預估 27 年
N2 年產能達 2.4 倍，台灣南科及美國亞利桑那 N3 新廠陸續量產，AI 加
速器與 Agentic AI 需求續增，EPS 151.11 元。 
3. 目前台積電股價交易在 2,440 元(16.1X 27(F) PER)，位於近三年歷史本益
比參考區間 12~26x 中間偏下位置，因 2 奈米量產爬坡以及海外廠折舊稀
釋，27 年毛利率將小幅衰退，本益比由中間偏上之 23 倍下調至 22 倍，
因調高 27 年獲利預估，故維持強力買進投資建議，目標價由3,220 元(23 
x 修正前 FY27E EPS)調升至 3,320 元(22 x 修正後 FY27E EPS)。 
重點更新 
台積電 3Q26 受惠 AI 及先進製程需求強勁，優於預期且創歷史新高 
N2 製程自 4Q25 量產後持續擴大產能，帶動 3Q26 HPC 與智慧型手機營收分
別季增 22%及 10%，占整體營收比重達 68%及 21%。在先進製程占比提升及
晶圓出貨量增加帶動下，營收 1.494 兆元(+17.6% QoQ) 。雖然 N3 產能利用
率維持高檔，加上整體營運規模持續擴大，惟 N2 初期量產仍面臨較高的製造
成 本 與 折 舊 負 擔 ， 形 成 稀 釋 ， 但 稀 釋 程 度 低 於 我 們 原 先 預 期 ， 毛 利 率
66.4%(-1.3ppts QoQ) ，營業利益 8,764.45 億元(+15.1% QoQ) ，稅後淨利
7,633.41 億元(+8.0% QoQ)，EPS 29.43 元，優於預期且創歷史新高。  
預期台積電 4Q26 受惠 AI 需求持續擴張，N2 放量推升營運再創新高 
隨 CSP 持續增加 AI 基礎建設投資，AI 加速器、Agentic AI 相關 CPU 及網通
晶片對先進製程的需求持續增加，上修 4Q26 營收預估 9.5%至 1.699 兆元
(+13.8% QoQ)。N2 處於快速擴產階段，初期量產成本及新增折舊仍對毛利率
形成壓力，加上海外晶圓廠持續稀釋毛利率，但先進製程需求強勁有助維持高
產能利用率，且 N3 製程量產規模擴大，小幅上調毛利率預估 0.1 個百分點至
66.3%(-0.1ppts QoQ)。調高營業利益至 9,911.86 億元(+13.1% QoQ)，上修
稅後淨利至 8,315.29 億元(+8.9% QoQ)，EPS 32.07 元，營運續創歷史新高。

### Page 2

報告內容 
台積電 3Q26 受惠 AI 及先進製程需求強勁，優於預期且創歷史新高 
受惠 AI 相關需求持續強勁 ，Agentic AI 逐步由傳統聊天機器人朝 AI 助理發展 ， 帶動AI 加速器、
CPU 及網通晶片需求同步增加，加上智慧型手機新產品備貨需求，以及 N2 製程自 4Q25 量產
後持續擴大產能，帶動 3Q26 HPC 與智慧型手機營收分別季增 22%及 10%，占整體營收比重
達 68%及 21%。以製程別觀察，N2 營收占晶圓營收比重由 2Q26 的 3%提升至 8%，N3 亦由
30%提升至 33%，N7 及以下先進製程合計占比達 79%，顯示先進製程需求持續成為主要成長
動能，3Q26 以 12 吋約當晶圓計算之出貨量季增 3.2%至 447.6 萬片。 
在先進製程占比提升及晶圓出貨量增加帶動下 ， 營收1.494 兆元(+17.6% QoQ)， 優於預期3.0%。
雖然 N3 產能利用率維持高檔，加上整體營運規模持續擴大，惟 N2 初期量產仍面臨較高的製
造成本與折舊負擔 ， 形成稀釋 ，但稀釋程度低於我們原先預期 ，毛利率 66.4%(-1.3ppts QoQ)。
隨 N2、A14 等先進製程持續開發，研發費用增加至 889.79 億元，推升營業費用至 1,159.34
億元(+17.1% QoQ)，營業利益 8,764.45 億元(+15.1% QoQ)，營業利益率 58.7%，稅後淨利
7,633.41 億元(+8.0% QoQ)，EPS 29.43 元，優於預期且創歷史新高。

預期台積電 4Q26 受惠 AI 需求持續擴張，N2 放量推升營運再創新高 
AI 相關需求延續強勁成長趨勢，隨 CSP 持續增加 AI 基礎建設投資，AI 加速器、Agentic AI 相
關CPU 及網通晶片對先進製程的需求持續增加 ， 加上N2製程於新竹及高雄廠區持續擴大產能，
預期將成為 4Q26 主要營收成長動能。雖然部分消費性電子及價格敏感型終端市場受到零組件
價格上漲與總體經濟不確定性影響，需求相對疲弱，但 HPC 與智慧型手機對 N2、N3 等先進
製程的需求仍維持強勁，且台積電持續透過 N5 設備轉換支援 N3，以及 N7、N5、N3 跨製程
節點產能優化，提高晶圓產出與產能利用效率。

### Page 3

上修 4Q26 營收預估 9.5%至 1.699 兆元(+13.8% QoQ) 。N2 處於快速擴產階段，初期量產成
本及新增折舊仍對毛利率形成壓力 ， 加上海外晶圓廠生產成本相對較高 ， 持續帶來毛利率稀釋，
但先進製程需求強勁有助維持高產能利用率，且 N3 製程量產規模擴大與生產效率改善有助降
低單位製造成本，小幅上調毛利率預估 0.1 個百分點至 66.3%(-0.1ppts QoQ) 。隨先進製程研
發投入增加、全球產能擴張及營運規模持續成長，調升營業費用預估 16.9%至 1,364.36 億元
(+17.7% QoQ)，調高營業利益至 9,911.86 億元(+13.1% QoQ)，營業利益率 58.3%，上修稅
後淨利預估 7.3%至 8,315.29 億元(+8.9% QoQ)，EPS 32.07 元，營運續創歷史新高。

先進製程與 CoWoS 持續供不應求，預期台積電 26 年營運創新高 
隨全球 CSP 持續擴大 AI 資料中心建設，AI 加速器、客製化 ASIC 及高速網通晶片需求持續增
加，加上 Agentic AI 逐步由聊天機器人朝具備自主執行能力的 AI 助理發展，進一步帶動 CPU
及先進製程運算晶片需求，推升台積電 HPC 業務持續成長。製程方面，N3 持續受惠 HPC、智
慧型手機及 AI 相關需求，產能利用率維持高檔，N2 自 4Q25 正式量產後，於 26 年持續擴大
新竹及高雄廠區產能，逐步提高先進製程營收貢獻。CoWoS 先進封裝需求持續強勁，台積電
已具備 5 倍光罩尺寸封裝量產能力，並朝 9 倍及 14 倍光罩尺寸推進，以滿足 AI 加速器晶片尺
寸與 HBM 整合需求持續提升的趨勢，預估 26 年年底 CoWoS 月產能達 14 萬片。 
在先進製程及先進封裝需求同步成長下 ，上修26 年營收預估3.5%至5.598 兆元(+47.0% YoY)。
受惠 N3 製程產能利用率提升、產品組合朝高階製程轉移，以及整體營運規模擴大帶來的製造
效率與成本改善，有助抵銷N2 初期量產及海外晶圓廠成本稀釋，其中海外廠預估對26 年全年
毛利率造成約 2 個百分點影響，維持資本支出 620 億美金之預估，上調毛利率預估 0.2 個百分
點至 66.7%(+6.8ppts YoY) 。調升營業利益預估 3.4%至 3.286 兆元(+69.8% YoY) ，調高稅後
淨利預估 3.8%至 2.874 兆元(+67.6% YoY)，EPS 110.83 元，營運創歷史新高。

### Page 4

預期台積電 27 年 N2 全面放量與全球產能陸續開出，營運再創新高 
隨全球 CSP 持續增加 AI 基礎建設投資 ，AI 運算需求由訓練逐步延伸至推論及 Agentic AI 應用，
帶動 AI 加速器、CPU 及網通晶片需求持續增加，使先進製程及先進封裝需求維持成長。製程
方面，我們預估 27 年 N2 全年產能較 26 年增加至 2.4 倍，主要由智慧型手機及 HPC/AI 客戶
需求推動，同時透過製造效率提升增加有效晶圓產出。N3 方面，受惠AI 及 HPC 客戶多年期需
求能見度提高，持續增加全球 N3 產能，南科新增 N3 晶圓廠預計 1H27 量產，且因客戶需求
持續增加，決定再新增一座 N3 廠，預計 1H28 量產；美國方面，亞利桑那第二廠已完成廠房
建設，目前進入設備搬入及安裝階段，預計 3Q27 開始量產，時程較原先規劃提前，日本 P2
則規劃於 28 年量產。隨 AI 加速器封裝尺寸持續增加，CoWoS 朝 9 倍、14 倍光罩尺寸發展，
CoPoS 亦持續開發，有望支援未來更大尺寸及更高整合度的 AI 晶片需求，27 年年底 CoWoS
月產能由原先預估之 19 萬片/月上修至 20 萬片/月。 
因先進製程持續供不應求，上修 27 年營收預估 12.3%至 8.143 兆元(+45.5% YoY)。為了因應
擴產需求，27 年資本支出由原先預估至 800 億美金上修至 850 億美金(+37.1% YoY) ，N2 持
續快速擴產、新增產能折舊增加，以及亞利桑那第二廠開始量產，海外晶圓廠成本稀釋壓力將
逐步提高，但上調 N3 及 N2 產能利用率，且先進製程占比提升有助維持產品組合優勢，調高
毛利率預估 0.5 個百分點至 66.5%(-0.1ppts YoY) ，調升營業利益預估 9.2%至 4.673 兆元
(+42.2% YoY)，營業利益率 57.4%，上修稅後淨利預估 7.8%至 3.918 兆元(+36.3% YoY)，EPS 
151.11 元，營運續創新高。
